# Federal Act on Debt Enforcement and Bankruptcy

English is not an official language of the Swiss Confederation. This translation is provided for information purposes only and has no legal force.

(DEBA)[^t-1]

of 11 April 1889 (Status as of 1 January 2025)

[^t-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

The Federal Assembly of the Swiss Confederation,

based on Article 64 of the Federal Constitution[^ingress-1],[^ingress-2]

decrees:

[^ingress-1]: [BS **1** 3]. This provision corresponds to Art. 122 para. 1 of the Federal Constitution of 18 April 1999 ([SR 101](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/4715)).
[^ingress-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 24 March 2000, in force since 1 Jan. 2001 ([AS 2000 2531](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2000/401); [BBl 1999 9126](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1999/1_9126_8486_8077)[9547](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1999/1_9547_8886_8458)).

## Title One General Provisions

### I. Organisation

### A. Debt enforcement and bankruptcy districts

[^tit1-lvlI-lvlA-1]

[^tit1-lvlI-lvlA-1]: All articles were provided with margin titles by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997, ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

#### Art. 1

<sup>1</sup> The territory of each canton shall form one or more districts for the purpose of debt enforcement and bankruptcy proceedings.

<sup>2</sup> The cantons shall determine the number and size of these districts.

<sup>3</sup> A bankruptcy district may comprise two or more debt enforcement districts.

### B. Debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices

### 1. Organisation

#### Art. 2

[^2-1]

<sup>1</sup> Each debt enforcement district has a debt enforcement office, which shall be headed by a debt enforcement officer.

<sup>2</sup> Each bankruptcy district has a bankruptcy office, which shall be headed by a bankruptcy officer.

<sup>3</sup> Each debt enforcement officer and each bankruptcy officer shall have a deputy who shall replace him if he recuses himself or is unable to manage the office.

<sup>4</sup> The debt enforcement office and the bankruptcy office may be merged and headed by the same public official.

<sup>5</sup> The cantons shall otherwise decide on how the debt enforcement and the bankruptcy offices are organised.

[^2-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Remuneration

#### Art. 3

[^3-1]

The remuneration of debt enforcement and bankruptcy officers and their deputies is a matter for the cantons.

[^3-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### C. Legal assistance

#### Art. 4

[^4-1]

<sup>1</sup> The debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices shall carry out official acts at the request of other public offices, non-official bankruptcy administrations, commissioners and liquidators from another district.

<sup>2</sup> With the consent of the office that has jurisdiction, debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices, non-official bankruptcy administrations, commissioners and liquidators may also perform official acts outside their district. However, only the office at the place where the act must be carried out is competent to serve debt enforcement notices other than by post or to conduct distraint proceedings and public auctions or to request assistance from the police

[^4-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### C<sup>bis</sup>. Proceedings with the same subject matter

#### Art. 4a

[^4a-1]

<sup>1</sup> In the case of bankruptcies and composition proceedings that have the same subject matter, the compulsory execution bodies, supervisory authorities and courts involved shall coordinate their actions as far as possible.

<sup>2</sup> The bankruptcy and composition courts involved and the supervisory authorities may, by mutual agreement, designate a single jurisdiction for all proceedings.

[^4a-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 21 June 2013, in force since 1 Jan. 2014 ([AS 2013 4111](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2013/757); [BBl 2010 6455](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2010/1144)).

### D. Liability

### 1. Principle

#### Art. 5

[^5-1]

<sup>1</sup> The canton shall be liable for any loss or damage caused unlawfully by debt enforcement and bankruptcy officers and their employees, their auxiliaries, non-official bankruptcy administrations, commissioners, liquidators, supervisory and judicial authorities and the police in the fulfilment of the duties assigned to them by this Act.

<sup>2</sup> The person suffering loss or damage has no claim against the person at fault.

<sup>3</sup> Recourse by the canton against the person who has caused the loss or damage is governed by cantonal law.

<sup>4</sup> Where the severity of the loss or damage justifies it, there is also a right to satisfaction.

[^5-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Statute of limitations

#### Art. 6

[^6-1]

<sup>1</sup> A claim for damages shall become time-barred three years from the day on which the person suffering damage became aware of the damage, but in any case ten years from the day on which the harmful behaviour occurred or ceased.

<sup>2</sup> If the person who caused the damage has committed a criminal offence through his conduct, the claim for damages shall become time-barred at the earliest when the right to prosecute becomes time-barred. If this no longer applies as a result of a first-instance criminal judgment, the claim shall become time-barred at the earliest three years after notice of the judgment was served.

[^6-1]: Amended by Annex No 4 of the FA of 15 June 2018 (Revision of the Law on Limitation Periods), in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2018 5343](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2018/807); [BBl 2014 235](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2014/65)).

### 3 Jurisdiction of the Federal Supreme Court

#### Art. 7

[^7-1]

Where an action for damages is filed on the grounds of unlawful conduct by the cantonal supervisory authorities of second instance or of the cantonal composition court of second instance, the Federal Supreme Court has exclusive jurisdiction.

[^7-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### E. Records and registers

### 1. Keeping, probative value and correction

#### Art. 8

[^8-1]

<sup>1</sup> The debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices shall keep a record of their official activities and of the requests and declarations they receive; they keep the registers.

<sup>2</sup> The records and registers are conclusive in their content until proven otherwise.

<sup>3</sup> The debt enforcement office shall correct an incorrect entry *ex officio* or at the request of the person concerned.

[^8-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Right to inspect

#### Art. 8a

[^8a-1]

<sup>1</sup> Any person who can credibly demonstrate an interest may inspect the records and registers of the debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices and obtain extracts from them.

<sup>2</sup> An interest is deemed to be credible in particular if the request for information is made in direct connection with the conclusion or performance of a contract.

<sup>3</sup>

The offices shall not provide third parties with information about debt enforcement proceedings if:

- a. the debt enforcement proceedings are null and void or have been annulled as the result of an appeal or a court decision[^8a-2];
- b. the debtor succeeded with an action for restitution of money paid without legal cause;
- c. the creditor has withdrawn the debt enforcement proceedings;
- d.[^8a-3] the debtor has submitted a request not to provide information after three months has elapsed since the summons for payment was served, unless the creditor provides proof that proceedings to set aside the objection (Arts 79-84) were initiated in good time after a period of 20 days set by the debt enforcement office has elapsed; if this proof is subsequently provided or if the debt enforcement continues, it will be brought to the attention of third parties again.

<sup>4</sup> The right to inspect of third parties expires five years after the conclusion of the proceedings. Judicial and administrative authorities may still request extracts in the interests of proceedings pending before them.

[^8a-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^8a-2]: Term pursuant to Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); BBl 2006 7221).
[^8a-3]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 2016, in force since 1 Jan. 2019 ([AS 2018 4583](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2018/721); [BBl 2015 3209](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2015/692)[5785](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2015/1322)).

### F. Safekeeping of money or valuables

#### Art. 9

The debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices shall hand over sums of money, securities and valuables which are not disposed of within three days of receipt to the official depositary.

### G. Duty of recusal

#### Art. 10

[^10-1]

<sup>1</sup>

Officers and employees of the debt enforcement and the bankruptcy offices, and members of the supervisory authorities may not carry out official acts:

- 1\. relating to their own affairs;
- 2\.[^10-2] in matters relating to their spouses, registered partners or persons with whom they are in *de facto* cohabitation;
- 2<sup>bis</sup>.[^10-3] in matters relating to relatives and in-laws in direct line or in collateral line to the third degree;
- 3\. in matters relating to a person for whom they act as a legal representative, hold a power of attorney or are an employee;
- 4\. in matters in which they may not be impartial for other reasons.

<sup>2</sup> A debt enforcement or bankruptcy officer who must recuse himself shall immediately forward any request he receives to his deputy and notify the creditors of this by standard post.

[^10-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^10-2]: Amended by Annex No 16 of the Same-Sex Partnership Act of 18 June 2004, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2005 5685](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2005/782); [BBl 2003 1288](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2003/194)).
[^10-3]: Inserted by Annex No 16 of the Same-Sex Partnership Act of 18 June 2004, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2005 5685](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2005/782); [BBl 2003 1288](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2003/194)).

### H. Officers and employees of the debt enforcement and the bankruptcy offices

[^tit1-lvlI-lvlH-1]

[^tit1-lvlI-lvlH-1]: Amended by No I 2 of the FA of 18 March 2022 on Combating Bankruptcy Fraud, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2023 628](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/628); [BBl 2019 5193](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2019/1847)).

#### Art. 11

[^11-1]

<sup>1</sup> The officers and employees of the debt enforcement and the bankruptcy offices may not enter into any legal transactions for their own account in relation to claims to be enforced or goods to be sold by the office. Legal acts that breach this provision are null and void.

<sup>2</sup> Bankruptcy officers are obliged to report to the prosecution authorities any felonies or misdemeanours that they or their subordinates discover in the course of their official duties or that are reported to them and for which there are specific grounds for suspicion.[^11-2]

<sup>3</sup> Subject to the same conditions, any person working for the bankruptcy office is also entitled to report offences to be prosecuted *ex officio* to the prosecution authorities.[^11-3]

[^11-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^11-2]: Inserted by No I 2 of the FA of 18 March 2022 on Combating Bankruptcy Fraud, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2023 628](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/628); [BBl 2019 5193](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2019/1847)).
[^11-3]: Inserted by No I 2 of the FA of 18 March 2022 on Combating Bankruptcy Fraud, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2023 628](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/628); [BBl 2019 5193](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2019/1847)).

### I. Payments made to the debt enforcement office

#### Art. 12

<sup>1</sup> The debt enforcement office must accept payments for the account of the creditor seeking enforcement.

<sup>2</sup> The debt is cancelled by payment to the debt enforcement office.

### K. Supervisory authorities

### 1. Cantonal

### a. Appointment

#### Art. 13

<sup>1</sup> Each canton must appoint a supervisory authority to supervise the debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices.

<sup>2</sup> The cantons may also appoint lower supervisory authorities for one or more districts.

### b. Management audit and disciplinary measures

#### Art. 14

<sup>1</sup> The supervisory authority must audit the management of each office at least once a year.

<sup>2</sup>

The following disciplinary measures may be taken against an officer or employee:[^14-1]

- 1\. reprimand;
- 2\.[^14-2] fine not exceeding 1000 francs;
- 3\. suspension for a maximum of six months;
- 4\. removal from office.

[^14-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^14-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Federal Council

#### Art. 15

[^15-1]

<sup>1</sup> The Federal Council shall exercise oversight over the debt enforcement and bankruptcy matters and ensure the uniform application of this Act.

<sup>2</sup> It shall issue the ordinances and regulations necessary for the implementation of this Act.

<sup>3</sup> It may issue directives to the cantonal supervisory authorities and request annual reports from them.

<sup>4</sup> …[^15-2]

<sup>5</sup> It shall coordinate electronic communication between the debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices, the land register and commercial register offices, the courts and the public.[^15-3]

[^15-1]: Amended by Annex No 6 of the Federal Supreme Court Act of 17 June 2005, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2006 1205](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2006/218); [BBl 2001 4202](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2001/731)).
[^15-2]: Repealed by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, with effect from 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^15-3]: Inserted by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).

### L. Fees

#### Art. 16

<sup>1</sup> The Federal Council shall set the tariff of fees.

<sup>2</sup> Documents issued in debt enforcement and bankruptcy proceedings are exempt from stamp duty.

### M. Appeal

### 1. To the supervisory authority

#### Art. 17

<sup>1</sup> With the exception of cases in which this Act requires court action, an appeal may be filed with the supervisory authority against any order of a debt enforcement or bankruptcy office due to infringement of the law or unreasonableness.[^17-1]

<sup>2</sup> The appeal must be filed within ten days of the date on which the appellant received notice of the order.

<sup>3</sup> An appeal may be filed at any time on the grounds of denial of justice or unjustified delay.

<sup>4</sup> The office may reconsider its order at any time before the deadline on which it must submit its response to the appeal. If it issues a new order, it shall give notice of the same to the parties immediately and inform the supervisory authority.[^17-2]

[^17-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^17-2]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. To the higher supervisory authority

#### Art. 18

[^18-1]

<sup>1</sup> The decision of a lower supervisory authority may be referred within ten days of notice thereof being given to the higher cantonal supervisory authority.

<sup>2</sup> An appeal may be filed at any time with the higher cantonal supervisory on the grounds of denial of justice or unjustified delay by a lower supervisory authority.

[^18-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 3. To the Federal Supreme Court

#### Art. 19

[^19-1]

An appeal to the Federal Supreme Court is governed by the Federal Supreme Court Act of 17 June 2005[^19-2].

[^19-1]: Amended by Annex No 6 of the Federal Supreme Court Act of 17 June 2005, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2006 1205](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2006/218); [BBl 2001 4202](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2001/731)).
[^19-2]: [SR 173.110](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5575)

### 4. Deadlines for filing an appeal in debt enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange

#### Art. 20

In cases relating to debt enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange, the period allowed for filing an appeal in first and second instance amounts to five days only; the authority must decide on the appeal within five days.

### 5. Proceedings before cantonal supervisory authorities

[^tit1-lvlI-lvlM-lvl5-1]

[^tit1-lvlI-lvlM-lvl5-1]: Amended by Annex No 6 of the Federal Supreme Court Act of 17 June 2005, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2006 1205](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2006/218); [BBl 2001 4202](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2001/731)).

#### Art. 20a

[^20a-1]

<sup>1</sup> …[^20a-2]

<sup>2</sup>

The following provisions apply to the procedure before the cantonal supervisory authorities:[^20a-3]

- 1\. The supervisory authorities must in every case in which they act in this capacity designate themselves as such and, if appropriate, as the higher or lower supervisory authority.
- 2\. The supervisory authority establishes the facts of the case *ex officio*. It may require the assistance of the parties in this process and need not consider their request if they refuse to provide assistance that is requested and can reasonably be expected of them.
- 3\.[^20a-4] The supervisory authority evaluates the evidence freely; subject to Article 22, it may not go beyond the parties’ requests.
- 4\. The decision shall be justified, contain instructions on rights of appeal, and written notice thereof shall be given to the parties, the office concerned and any other persons concerned.
- 5\.[^20a-5] The proceedings are free of charge. In the case of malicious or vexatious litigation, the party concerned or their agent may be required to pay a fine not exceeding 1500 francs and the fees and outlays.

<sup>3</sup> The cantons shall otherwise regulate the procedure.

[^20a-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^20a-2]: Repealed by Annex No 6 of the Federal Supreme Court Act of 17 June 2005, with effect from 1 Jan. 2007 (AS 2006 1205; BBl 2001 4202).
[^20a-3]: Amended by Annex No 6 of the Federal Supreme Court Act of 17 June 2005, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2006 1205](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2006/218); [BBl 2001 4202](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2001/731)).
[^20a-4]: Amended by No I 6 of the Federal Assembly O of 20 Dec. 2006 on the Adaptation of Legislation to the Provisions of the Federal Supreme Court Act and the Administrative Court Act, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2006 5599](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2006/826); [BBl 2006 7759](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/974)).
[^20a-5]: Inserted by Annex No 6 of the Federal Supreme Court Act of 17 June 2005, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2006 1205](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2006/218); [BBl 2001 4202](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2001/731)).

### 6 Decision on appeal

#### Art. 21

An authority that upholds an appeal shall order the contested act to be quashed or corrected; it shall order the execution of acts that the official refuses to carry out or delays without justification.

### N. Null and void orders

#### Art. 22

[^22-1]

<sup>1</sup> If orders breach regulations issued in the public interest or in the interests of persons not party to the proceedings, these rulings shall be null and void. Irrespective of whether an appeal has been filed, the supervisory authorities shall declare the rulings null and void *ex officio*.

<sup>2</sup> The office may replace an order that has been declared null and void with a new order. If proceedings under paragraph 1 are pending before the supervisory authority, the office may do this at any time before the deadline on which it must submit its response.

[^22-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### O. Cantonal implementing provisions

### 1. Judicial authorities

#### Art. 23

[^23-1]

The cantons shall designate the judicial authorities that are responsible for making the decision assigned to a court under this Act.

[^23-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Official depositaries

#### Art. 24

The cantons shall designate the institutions that are required to accept deposits in the cases provided for in this Act (official depositaries). The cantons are liable for the deposits held by these institutions.

### 3. …

#### Art. 25

[^25-1]

[^25-1]: Repealed by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, with effect from 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).

### 4. Public law consequences of unsuccessful distraint proceedings or bankruptcy

#### Art. 26

[^26-1]

<sup>1</sup> The cantons may, unless federal law applies, impose consequences under public law (such as declaring a person unfit for public office or to carry on a profession or activities requiring authorisation) to unsuccessful distraint proceedings or a declaration of bankruptcy. These consequences may not include a withdrawal of the right to vote or be elected, or the publication of unpaid debt certificates.

<sup>2</sup> The legal consequences must be revoked if the bankruptcy is annulled, if all creditors holding unpaid debt certificates have been repaid in full or their claims have become time-barred.

<sup>3</sup> If the only creditor to sustain a loss is the debtor’s spouse or registered partner, no consequences under public law may be imposed for unsuccessful distraint proceedings or bankruptcy.[^26-2]

[^26-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^26-2]: Amended by Annex No 16 of the Same-Sex Partnership Act of 18 June 2004, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2005 5685](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2005/782); [BBl 2003 1288](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2003/194)).

### 5. Representation in enforcement proceedings

#### Art. 27

[^27-1]

<sup>1</sup> Any person with legal capacity to act is authorised to represent other persons in enforcement proceedings. This also applies to professional representation. The cantons may prohibit a person from acting as a professional representative for good cause.

<sup>2</sup> The costs of representation in proceedings before the debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices may not be charged to the other party.

[^27-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 25 Sept. 2015 (Commercial Representation in Enforcement Proceedings), in force since 1 Jan. 2018 ([AS 2016 3643](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2016/612); [BBl 2014 8669](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2014/1878)).

### P. Notice of the cantonal system of organisation

#### Art. 28

[^28-1]

<sup>1</sup> The cantons shall provide the Federal Council with details of the debt enforcement and bankruptcy districts and of the system of organisation of the debt enforcement and the bankruptcy offices and of the authorities that they have appointed in implementation of this Act.

<sup>2</sup> The Federal Council shall ensure that this information is made public in an appropriate manner.

[^28-1]: Amended by No I 6 of the Federal Assembly Ordinance of 20 Dec. 2006 on the adaptation of decrees to the provisions of the Federal Supreme Court Act and the Administrative Court Act, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2006 5599](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2006/826); [BBl 2006 7759](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/974)).

### Q. ...

#### Art. 29

[^29-1]

[^29-1]: Repealed by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, with effect from 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).

### R. Special enforcement proceedings

#### Art. 30

[^30-1]

<sup>1</sup> This Act does not apply to the enforcement proceedings against cantons, districts and communes where special federal or cantonal regulations apply.

<sup>2</sup> The provisions of other federal laws on special enforcement proceedings are also reserved.

[^30-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### S. International treaties and private international law

#### Art. 30a

[^30a-1]

International treaties and the provisions of the Federal Act of 18 December 1987[^30a-2] on Private International Law (PILA) are reserved.

[^30a-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^30a-2]: [SR 291](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/6186)

### II. Miscellaneous Regulations

### A. Deadlines

### 1. General

#### Art. 31

[^31-1]

The computation of and compliance with deadlines are governed by the provisions of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 December 2008[^31-2] (CPC), unless this Act provides otherwise.

[^31-1]: Amended by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^31-2]: [SR 272](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/6136)

### 2. Compliance

#### Art. 32

[^32-1]

<sup>1</sup> …[^32-2]

<sup>2</sup> A deadline is also complied with if a debt enforcement or bankruptcy office without jurisdiction is seised before its expiry; this office shall transfer the case immediately to the office that has jurisdiction.[^32-3]

<sup>3</sup> …[^32-4]

<sup>4</sup> In the case of written submissions that contain errors that can be corrected, an opportunity must be given to correct them.

[^32-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^32-2]: Repealed by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, with effect from 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^32-3]: Amended by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^32-4]: Repealed by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, with effect from 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).

### 3. Amendment and reinstatement

#### Art. 33

<sup>1</sup> The deadlines laid down in this Act may not be amended by agreement.

<sup>2</sup> If a party to proceedings lives abroad or if he must be notified by public notice, a longer deadline may be granted or an existing deadline extended.[^33-1]

<sup>3</sup> A party to proceedings may waive his right to claim non-compliance with a deadline if the deadline is imposed exclusively in his interest.[^33-2]

<sup>4</sup> Any person who is prevented from acting within a deadline due to circumstances beyond his control may request the supervisory authority or the judicial authority with jurisdiction over the case to reinstate the deadline. From the date on which those circumstances no longer apply, he must carry out the required legal act for the competent authority within the same period as that which has expired and file an application stating the grounds for reinstating the deadline.[^33-3]

[^33-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^33-2]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^33-3]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### A<sup>bis</sup>. Electronic transmission

#### Art. 33a

[^33a-1]

<sup>1</sup> A submission may be filed electronically with the debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices and the supervisory authorities.

<sup>2</sup> The submission must be provided with a qualified electronic signature in accordance with the Federal Act of 18 March 2016[^33a-2] on Electronic Signatures. The Federal Council may make exceptions for mass procedures.

<sup>3</sup> The relevant time for compliance with a deadline is that at which the receipt is issued that confirms that all the steps necessary for transmission have been completed by the party or their representative.

<sup>4</sup>

The Federal Council shall regulate:

- a. the format of the submission and its enclosures;
- b. the method of transmission;
- c. the requirements for requesting the submission of documents in paper form in the event of technical problems.

[^33a-1]: Inserted by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)). Amended by Annex II 6 of the FA of 18 March 2016 on the electronic signature, in force since 1 Jan. 2017 ([AS 2016 4651](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2016/752); [BBl 2014 1001](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2014/171)).
[^33a-2]: [SR 943.03](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/11301)

### B. Service

### 1. In writing and electronically

#### Art. 34

[^34-1]

<sup>1</sup> Notices, orders and decisions of the debt enforcement and bankruptcy offices and the supervisory authorities shall be served by registered mail or in any other way against confirmation of receipt, unless this Act provides otherwise

<sup>2</sup>

With the consent of the person concerned, notices, orders and decisions may be sent electronically. They must bear an electronic signature in accordance with the Federal Act of 18 March 2016[^34-2] on Electronic Signatures. The Federal Council shall regulate:

- a. the signature to be used;
- b. the format of notices, orders and decisions and their enclosures;
- c. the method of transmission;
- d. the date on which the notice, order or decision is deemed to have been served.[^34-3]

[^34-1]: Amended by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^34-2]: [SR 943.03](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/11301)
[^34-3]: Amended by Annex II 6 of the FA of 18 March 2016 on Electronic Signatures, in force since 1 Jan. 2017 ([AS 2016 4651](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2016/752); [BBl 2014 1001](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2014/171)).

### 2. By public notice

#### Art. 35

<sup>1</sup> Public notice shall be given in the Swiss Official Gazette of Commerce and in the relevant official cantonal gazette. For the calculation of deadlines and determining the legal consequences of the notice, publication in the Swiss Official Gazette of Commerce is decisive.[^35-1]

<sup>2</sup> If the circumstances so require, notice may also be given in other publications or by public proclamation.

[^35-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### C. Suspensive effect

#### Art. 36

No appeal of any form shall have suspensive effect unless this is specifically ordered by the authority to which it is addressed or its presiding official. If such an order is made, immediate notice must be given to the parties.

### D. Terms

#### Art. 37

[^37-1]

<sup>1</sup> The term "mortgage" in this Act comprises mortgages, mortgage certificates, mortgage rights under the previous law, real burdens and any preferential rights to specific immovable properties as well as the pledge on the appurtenances of an immovable property.[^37-2]

<sup>2</sup> The term "pledge of chattels" also includes the pledge of livestock, the special lien and the pledge on claims and other rights.

<sup>3</sup> The term "pledge" comprises both the mortgage and the pledge of chattels.

[^37-1]: Amended by Art. 58 Final Title CC, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title CC; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).
[^37-2]: Amended by no II 4 of the FA of 11 Dec. 2009 (Register Mortgage Certificates and Other Amendments to Property Law), in force since 1 Jan. 2012 ([AS 2011 4637](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2011/666); [BBl 2007 5283](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2007/756)).

## Title Two Debt Enforcement

### I. Types of Debt Enforcement

### A. Object of debt enforcement and types of debt enforcement

#### Art. 38

<sup>1</sup> Debt enforcement is an enforcement procedure carried out to secure the payment of money or the provision of security.

<sup>2</sup> Debt enforcement begins with the service of the summons for payment and is continued either by way of distraint, proceedings to realise a pledge or bankruptcy.

<sup>3</sup> The debt enforcement officer decides which type of debt enforcement applies.

### B. Bankruptcy proceedings

### 1. Scope of application

#### Art. 39

<sup>1</sup>

Debt enforcement proceedings are continued by way of bankruptcy proceedings, either in the form of "ordinary bankruptcy proceedings" (Arts 159–176) or of "debt enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange" (Arts 177–189) if the debtor is registered in the Commercial Register as any of the following:

- 1\. as the owner of a sole proprietorship (Arts 934 and 935 CO[^39-1]);
- 2\. as a member of a general partnership (Art. 554 CO);
- 3\. as a member of a limited partnership with unlimited liability (Art. 596 CO);
- 4\. as a director of a partnership limited by shares (Art. 765 CO);
- 5\. [^39-2] ...
- 6\. as a general partnership (Art. 552 CO);
- 7\. as a limited partnership (Art. 594 CO);
- 8\. as a company limited by shares or a partnership limited by shares (Arts 620 and 764 CO);
- 9\. as a limited liability company (Art. 772 CO);
- 10\. as a cooperative (Art. 828 CO);
- 11\. as an association (Art. 60 Swiss Civil Code (CC)[^39-3]);
- 12\. as a foundation (Art. 80 CC);
- 13\.[^39-4] as an investment company with variable capital (Art. 36 Collective Investments Act of 23 June 2006[^39-5] (CISA));
- 14\.[^39-6] as a limited partnership for collective investment (Art. 98 CISA).[^39-7]

<sup>2</sup> …[^39-8]

<sup>3</sup> Registration takes effect on the day after notice thereof in published in the Swiss Official Gazette of Commerce.

[^39-1]: [SR 220](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5818)
[^39-2]: Repealed by Annex No 3 of the FA of 16 Dec. 2005 (Law on Limited Liability Companies and Amendments to the Law on Companies limited by Shares, Cooperatives, the Commercial Register and Business Names), with effect from 1 Jan. 2008 ([AS 2007 4791](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2007/685); [BBl 2002 3148](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2002/443), [2004 3969](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2004/680)).
[^39-3]: [SR 210](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5703)
[^39-4]: Inserted by Annex No II 3 of the Collective Investment Schemes Act of 23 June 2006, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2006 5379](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2006/822); [BBl 2005 6395](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2005/1000)).
[^39-5]: [SR 951.31](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/11533)
[^39-6]: Inserted by Annex No II 3 of the Collective Investment Schemes Act of 23 June 2006, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2006 5379](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2006/822); [BBl 2005 6395](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2005/1000)).
[^39-7]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^39-8]: Repealed by Art. 15 No 1 Final and Transitional Provisions to Titles XXIV–XXXIII CO ([AS 53 185](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/53/185_185_189); [BBl 1928 I 205](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1928/1_205_233_253), [1932 I 217](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1932/1_217__)).

### 2. Effective duration of the commercial register entry

#### Art. 40

<sup>1</sup> Persons who were registered in the Commercial Register remain subject to bankruptcy proceedings for six months following publication of their deletion in the Swiss Official Gazette of Commerce.

<sup>2</sup> If the creditor requests continuation before the expiry of this deadline or if he demands that a summons for payment be issued in debt enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange, the debt enforcement proceedings shall be continued by way of bankruptcy proceedings.[^40-1]

[^40-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### C. Debt enforcement proceedings to realise a pledge

#### Art. 41

[^41-1]

<sup>1</sup> For pledge-secured claims, the debt enforcement proceedings, including those against debtors subject to bankruptcy proceedings, are continued by proceedings to realise a pledge (Arts 151–158).

<sup>1bis</sup> If debt enforcement by distraint or through bankruptcy proceedings are initiated for a pledge-secured claim, the debtor may by filing an appeal (Art. 17) demand that the creditors first realise the pledge.

<sup>2</sup> In the case of interest or annuities secured by a mortgage, however, the creditor may choose either to have the pledge realised or, depending on the debtor’s legal personality, to initiate debt enforcement by distraint or bankruptcy. The provisions on debt enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange are reserved (Art. 177 para. 1).

[^41-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### D. Debt enforcement by distraint

#### Art. 42

[^42-1]

<sup>1</sup> In all other cases, debt enforcement is continued by way of distraint (Arts 89–150).

<sup>2</sup> If a debtor is subsequently registered in the Commercial Register, a pending request for continuation must still be executed by way of distraint unless the debtor has already been declared bankrupt.

[^42-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### E. Exceptions from bankruptcy proceedings

#### Art. 43

[^43-1]

Bankruptcy proceedings shall not be brought for:

- 1\.[^43-2] …
- 1<sup>bis</sup>.[^43-3] …
- 2\.[^43-4] regular family law maintenance and support contributions, and maintenance payments under the Same-Sex Partnership Act of 18 June 2004[^43-5];
- 3\. rights to the provision of security.

[^43-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^43-2]: Repealed by No I 2 of the FA of 18 March 2022 on Combating Bankruptcy Fraud, with effect from 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2023 628](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/628); [BBl 2019 5193](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2019/1847)).
[^43-3]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 3 Oct. 2003 ([AS 2004 2757](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2004/336); [BBl 2002 7107](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2002/1130)[7116](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2002/1132)). Repealed by No I 2 of the FA of 18 March 2022 on Combating Bankruptcy Fraud, with effect from 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2023 628](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/628); [BBl 2019 5193](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2019/1847)).
[^43-4]: Amended by Annex No 16 of the Same-Sex Partnership Act of 18 June 2004, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2005 5685](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2005/782); [BBl 2003 1288](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2003/194)).
[^43-5]: [SR 211.231](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5750)

### F. Reservation of special provisions

### 1. Realisation of seized items

#### Art. 44

[^44-1]

The realisation of items that have been seized under criminal or fiscal provisions or under the Federal Act of 18 December 2015[^44-2] on the Freezing and the Restitution of Illicit Assets held by Foreign Politically Exposed Persons shall be carried out in accordance with the relevant federal or cantonal provisions.

[^44-1]: Amended by Annex No 2 of the FA of 1 Oct. 2010 on the Restitution of Assets Obtained Unlawfully by Politically Exposed Persons, in force since 1 Feb. 2011 ([AS 2011 275](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2011/38); [BBl 2010 3309](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2010/596)).
[^44-2]: [SR 196.1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5693)

### 2. Claims by pawnbrokers

#### Art. 45

[^45-1]

The pursuit of pawnbrokers’ claims is governed by Article 910 of the Civil Code (Swiss Civil Code)[^45-2].

[^45-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^45-2]: [SR 210](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5703)

### II. Place of Debt Enforcement

### A. Ordinary place of debt enforcement

#### Art. 46

<sup>1</sup> Debt enforcement takes place at the debtor’s domicile.

<sup>2</sup> Debt enforcement against legal entities and companies registered in the Commercial Register takes place at their registered offices, or in the case of non-registered legal entities at the place where they are primarily managed.

<sup>3</sup> For debts arising from an agreement on joint ownership in undivided shares, in the absence of a representative, debt enforcement may take place against any of the joint owners at the place of their joint economic activity.[^46-1]

<sup>4</sup> Debt enforcement against a community of condominium owners takes place at the location of the property.[^46-2]

[^46-1]: Inserted by Art. 58 Final Title Civil Code, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title Civil Code; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).
[^46-2]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

#### Art. 47

[^47-1]

[^47-1]: Repealed by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### B. Special places of debt enforcement

### 1. Place of debt enforcement at current location

#### Art. 48

Debt enforcement against debtors of no fixed abode takes place where they are currently to be found.

### 2. Place of debt enforcement for a deceased person’s estate

#### Art. 49

[^49-1]

Provided the estate has not been distributed, a contractual community of property formed or bankruptcy liquidation ordered, debt enforcement against a deceased person’s estate may be carried out at the place and by the procedure which would have applied to the deceased person at the time of his death.

[^49-1]: Amended by Art. 58 Final Title Civil Code, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title Civil Code; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).

### 3. Place of debt enforcement for debtors living abroad

#### Art. 50

<sup>1</sup> In the case of debtors domiciled abroad who have a business establishment in Switzerland, enforcement take place in respect of liabilities incurred for the account of the business establishment at the location of the business establishment.

<sup>2</sup> Where debtors domiciled abroad have elected for a domicile in Switzerland in order to fulfil an obligation, enforcement may take place in respect of this obligation at the place of special domicile.

### 4. Debt enforcement where the object is located

#### Art. 51

<sup>1</sup> Where a pledge of chattels has been made to secure the claim, debt enforcement proceedings may either take place where required in accordance with Articles 46–50, or at the place where the pledge or the most valuable part thereof is located.[^51-1]

<sup>2</sup> For claims secured by a mortgage[^51-2], the debt enforcement proceedings may only take place[^51-3] where the mortgaged immovable property is located. If the debt enforcement proceedings related to two or more immovable properties located in different debt enforcement districts, debt enforcement must take place in the district in which the most valuable part of the immovable properties is located.

[^51-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^51-2]: Term pursuant to No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)). This amendment has been made throughout the text.
[^51-3]: Term pursuant to No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)). This amendment has been made throughout the text.

### 5. Place of debt enforcement in the case of attachment

#### Art. 52

If an attachment is made in respect of a claim, debt enforcement proceedings may also be instituted where the attached asset is located.[^52-1] The notice of impending bankruptcy and the declaration of bankruptcy may however only be issued where debt enforcement proceedings must normally take place.

[^52-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### C. Place of debt enforcement following change of domicile

#### Art. 53

If the debtor changes his domicile after receiving notice of distraint proceedings, notice of impending bankruptcy or a summons for payment in debt enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange, debt enforcement proceedings continue at their original location.

### D. Place of bankruptcy in the case of an absconding debtor

#### Art. 54

Bankruptcy proceedings are instituted against an absconding debtor at his last domicile.

### E. Unity of bankruptcy proceedings

#### Art. 55

Bankruptcy proceedings may only be instituted in Switzerland against a specific debtor at one place at any one time. Proceedings are deemed to have commenced at the place where the debtor is first declared bankrupt.

### III. Hours of Closure, Debt Enforcement Holidays and Stays of Enforcement

[^tit2-lvlIII-1]

[^tit2-lvlIII-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### A. Principles and terms

#### Art. 56

[^56-1]

<sup>1</sup>

Other than in attachment proceedings or in the case of urgent measures to preserve individual assets, enforcement procedures may not be carried out:

- 1\. during the hours of closure, namely between 8 p.m. and 7 a.m., and on Sundays and state-recognised public holidays;
- 2\. during debt enforcement holidays, namely the seven days before and seven days after Easter and Christmas, and from 15 July until 31 July; in the case of debt enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange there are no debt enforcement holidays;
- 3\. against a debtor who has been granted a stay of enforcement (Arts 57–62).

<sup>2</sup> The suspension of deadlines in actions under this Act that must be filed in a court are governed exclusively by the provisions thereon in the CPC[^56-2].[^56-3]

[^56-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 21 June 2013, in force since 1 Jan. 2014 ([AS 2013 4111](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2013/757); [BBl 2010 6455](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2010/1144)).
[^56-2]: [SR 272](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/6136)
[^56-3]: Inserted by No II 2 of the FA of 17 March 2023 (Improving Efficiency and Law Enforcement), in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2023 491](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/491); [BBl 2020 2697](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2020/653)).

### B. Stay of enforcement

### 1. Due to military, civil protection or civil defence service

[^tit2-lvlIII-lvlB-lvl1-1]

[^tit2-lvlIII-lvlB-lvl1-1]: Term pursuant to Annex No 4 des Civilian Service Act of 6 Oct. 1995, in force since 1 Oct. 1996 ([AS 1996 1445](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1996/1445_1445_1445); [BBl 1994 III 1609](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1994/3_1609_1579_1445)). This amendment has been made throughout the text.

### a. Duration

#### Art. 57

[^57-1]

<sup>1</sup> A stay of enforcement applies to debtors who are on military, civil protection or civil defence service, for the duration of such service.[^57-2]

<sup>2</sup> If the debtor completed at least 30 days of service without substantial interruption before discharge or leave, the stay of enforcement continues for two weeks following discharge or leave.

<sup>3</sup> Enforcement proceedings may be taken against debtors for regular family maintenance or support contributions during the stay of enforcement.[^57-3]

<sup>4</sup> Debtors who undertake military or civil defence service in terms of an employment contract with the Confederation or a canton are not entitled to a stay of enforcement.[^57-4]

[^57-1]: Amended by Art. 2 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 ([AS 1950 57](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1950/57_57_57); [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).
[^57-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^57-3]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^57-4]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### b. Third parties’ duty to provide information

#### Art. 57a

[^57a-1]

<sup>1</sup> If an enforcement procedure cannot be carried out because the debtor is on military, civil protection or civil defence service, adults in his household and if the debt enforcement notice is served in a place of business, the employees or if applicable the employer are obliged, subject to criminal penalties (Art. 324 No 5 SCC[^57a-2]) to notify the officer of the service address and the year of birth of the debtor.[^57a-3]

<sup>1bis</sup> The debt enforcement officer shall make those concerned aware of their obligations and of the criminal penalties in the event of their violation.[^57a-4]

<sup>2</sup> The command office concerned shall notify the debt enforcement office on request of the debtor’s discharge or leave.

<sup>3</sup> …[^57a-5]

[^57a-1]: Inserted by Art. 2 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).
[^57a-2]: [SR 311.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/6196)
[^57a-3]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^57a-4]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^57a-5]: Repealed by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, with effect from 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### c. Security under a mortgage

#### Art. 57b

[^57b-1]

<sup>1</sup> Where a debtor is enjoying a stay of enforcement due to military, civil protection or civil defence service, the duration of liability of a mortgage right for the interest of the claim secured by that mortgage right (Art. 818 para. 1 No 3 Swiss Civil Code[^57b-2]) is extended by the duration of the stay of enforcement.[^57b-3]

<sup>2</sup> In debt enforcement proceedings to realise a pledge, the summons for payment must also be served during the stay of enforcement if this has lasted for three months.

[^57b-1]: Inserted by Art. 2 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).
[^57b-2]: [SR 210](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5703)
[^57b-3]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### d. Inventory

#### Art. 57c

[^57c-1]

<sup>1</sup> Where a debtor is enjoying a stay of enforcement due to military, civil protection or civil defence service, the creditor may during the stay of enforcement demand that the debt enforcement office draw up a inventory with the effects set out in Article 164.[^57c-2] The creditor must however provide *prima facie* evidence that his claim exists and that it is endangered by acts of the debtor or third parties that aim to favour individual creditors to the detriment of others or to have a detrimental effect on the creditors in general.

<sup>2</sup> The drawing up of an inventory may be obviated by securing the applicant creditor’s claim.

[^57c-1]: Inserted by Art. 2 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).
[^57c-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### e. Lifting by the court

#### Art. 57d

[^57d-1]

A stay of enforcement due to military, civil protection or civil defence service may on application by a creditor be lifted with immediate effect by the court competent to set aside the objection in general or for individual claims if the creditor provides *prima facie* evidence that:[^57d-2]

- 1\. the debtor has deprived the creditors of access to assets or that he is making arrangements to favour certain creditors to the prejudice of others or to prejudice the creditors in general, or
- 2\.[^57d-3] the debtor is performing military, civil protection or civil defence service voluntarily and does not require the stay of enforcement for his financial subsistence, or
- 3\.[^57d-4] the debtor is performing military, civil protection or civil defence service voluntarily in order to evade his liabilities.

[^57d-1]: Inserted by Art. 2 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).
[^57d-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^57d-3]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^57d-4]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### f. Military, civil protection or civil defence service of the legal representative

#### Art. 57e

[^57e-1]

The provisions on the stay of enforcement also apply to persons and legal entities whose legal representative is performing military, civil protection or civil defence service, provided the person or entity is unable to appoint another representative.

[^57e-1]: Inserted by Art. 2 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)). Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Due to bereavement

#### Art. 58

[^58-1]

In the event of the death of the debtor’s spouse or registered partner, or of a relative or in-law of the debtor in direct line, or of a member of the debtor’s household, the debtor shall be entitled to a two-week stay of enforcement from the date of death.

[^58-1]: Amended by Annex No 16 of the Same-Sex Partnership Act of 18 June 2004, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2005 5685](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2005/782); [BBl 2003 1288](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2003/194)).

### 3. In debt enforcement proceedings for debts due from a deceased person’s estate

#### Art. 59

<sup>1</sup> In debt enforcement proceedings for debts due from a deceased person’s estate, a stay of enforcement applies from the testator’s date of death for the two subsequent weeks and during the period of reflection allowed for accepting or disclaiming the inheritance.[^59-1]

<sup>2</sup> Debt enforcement proceedings raised during the lifetime of the testator may be continued against the estate in accordance with Article 49.[^59-2]

<sup>3</sup> Proceedings may only be continued against the heirs if they involve debt enforcement proceedings to realise a pledge or if the deadlines given in Articles 110 and 111 for participating in distraint proceedings in a case of debt enforcement by distraint proceedings have already expired.

[^59-1]: Amended by Art. 2 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).
[^59-2]: Amended by Art. 58 Final Title Civil Code, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title Civil Code; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).

### 4. Due to arrest

#### Art. 60

If an arrested person who has no representative becomes subject to debt enforcement proceedings, the debt enforcement officer shall set him a deadline for appointing one.[^60-1] During this period, the arrested person is entitled to a stay of enforcement.

[^60-1]: Amended by Annex No 12 of the FA of 19 Dec. 2008 (Adult Protection Law, Law of Persons and Law of Children), in force since 1 Jan. 2013 ([AS 2011 725](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2011/114); [BBl 2006 7001](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/899)).

### 5. Due to serious illness

#### Art. 61

The debt enforcement officer may grant a seriously ill debtor a stay of enforcement for a specific period of time.

### 6. In the event of epidemics or national disasters

#### Art. 62

[^62-1]

In the event of an epidemic or a national disaster, as well as in times of war, the Federal Council or, with its consent, the cantonal government may decide to stay enforcement in a specific area or for certain sections of the population.

[^62-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### C. Effects on deadlines

#### Art. 63

[^63-1]

Debt enforcement holidays and stays of enforcement do not suspend the deadlines. However, if the deadline for the debtor, the creditor or the third party falls during the debt enforcement holiday or the stay of enforcement, the deadline shall be extended until the third day after the end of the holiday or stay. When calculating the three-day period, a Saturday, Sunday and officially recognised public holidays are not counted.

[^63-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### IV. Service of Debt Enforcement Notices

### A. On natural persons

#### Art. 64

<sup>1</sup> Debt enforcement notices shall be served on the debtor at his home or at the place where he carries out his profession. If he is not found there in person, service may be effected on an adult person belonging to his household or on an employee.

<sup>2</sup> If none of the persons mentioned are found, the debt enforcement notice must be handed to an official of the commune or police officer for delivery to the debtor.

### B. On legal entities, companies and undistributed estates

#### Art. 65

<sup>1</sup>

If debt enforcement proceedings are directed against a legal entity or a company, service shall be effected on the representative of the same. As such applies:

- 1\.[^65-1] for a commune, a canton or the Confederation, the president of the executive authority or the office designated by the executive authority;
- 2\.[^65-2] for a company limited by shares, a limited partnership, a limited liability company, a cooperative or an association registered in the Commercial Register, each member of the management or committee as well as each director or authorised signatory;
- 3\. for any other legal entity, the president of the administration or the administrator;
- 4\. for a general partnership or limited partnership, each partner authorised to represent the partnership and each authorised signatory.

<sup>2</sup> If the aforenamed persons cannot be found at their place of business, service may also be effected on another official or employee.

<sup>3</sup> If debt enforcement proceedings are directed against an undistributed estate, service shall be made on the representative appointed for the estate or, if no such representative is known, on one of the heirs.[^65-3]

[^65-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^65-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^65-3]: Inserted by Art. 58 Final Title CC, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title CC; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).

### C. If the debtor is domiciled abroad or in the case of impossibility of service

#### Art. 66

<sup>1</sup> If the debtor does not live at the place of debt enforcement, the debt enforcement notices shall be served on the person designated by the debtor or at the place designated by the debtor.

<sup>2</sup> In the absence of such a designation, service shall be effected through the debt enforcement office at the place of residence or by post.

<sup>3</sup> If the debtor resides abroad, service shall be effected through the local authorities or, if provided for by international treaties or if the recipient state consents, by the postal service.[^66-1]

<sup>4</sup>

Service shall be replaced by public notice if:

- 1\. the debtor's place of residence is unknown;
- 2\. the debtor persistently evades service;
- 3\. the debtor lives abroad and service in accordance with paragraph 3 is not possible within a reasonable period of time.[^66-2]

<sup>5</sup> …[^66-3]

[^66-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^66-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^66-3]: Repealed by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, with effect from 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### V. Initiating Debt Enforcement

### A. Request for debt enforcement

#### Art. 67

<sup>1</sup>

The request for debt enforcement must be filed in writing or made orally to the debt enforcement office. The following information must be provided:

- 1\. the name and place of residence of the creditor and, if applicable, his authorised representative and, if the creditor lives abroad, the domicile chosen by the creditor in Switzerland. If no clear domicile is indicated, it shall be assumed that the domicile is on the premises of the debt enforcement office;
- 2\.[^67-1] the name and place of residence of the debtor and, if applicable, his legal representative; in the case of a request for debt enforcements against a deceased’s estate, the heirs on whom service is to be effected must be specified;
- 3\. the amount of the claim or the amount for which security is demanded, in legal Swiss currency; in the case of interest-bearing claims, the interest rate and the date from which the interest is demanded;
- 4\. the document evidencing the claim and its date; in the absence of such, the grounds for the claim.

<sup>2</sup> For a claim secured by a pledge, the information specified in Article 151 must also be provided.

<sup>3</sup> The creditor shall be entitled to have receipt of the request for debt enforcement certified free of charge on request.

[^67-1]: Amended by Art. 58 Final Title CC, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title CC; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).

### B. Costs of debt enforcement

#### Art. 68

<sup>1</sup> The debtor shall bear the costs of debt enforcement. These must be paid in advance by the creditor. If advance payment is not made, the debt enforcement office may temporarily suspend the debt enforcement proceedings and notify the creditor thereof.

<sup>2</sup> The creditor is entitled to collect the debt enforcement costs in advance from the debtor’s payments.

### VI. Debt Enforcement Proceedings against a Spouse living under a Community of Property Regime

[^tit2-lvlVI-1]

[^tit2-lvlVI-1]: Originally No V<sup>bis</sup>. Inserted by Art. 15 no 3 Final and Transitional Provisions to Titles XXIV-XXXIII CO ([AS 53 185](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/53/185_185_189); [BBl 1928 I 205](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1928/1_205_233_253), [1932 I 217](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1932/1_217__)). Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### A. Service of the debt enforcement notice. Objection to the summons

#### Art. 68a

[^68a-1]

<sup>1</sup> If a spouse living under a community of property regime is the subject of debt enforcement proceedings, the summons for payment and all other debt enforcement notices must also be served on the other spouse; if it is only made known in the course of the proceedings that the debtor is subject to community of property, the debt enforcement office shall immediately effect service on the other spouse.

<sup>2</sup> Either spouse may file an objection to the summons.

<sup>3</sup> …[^68a-2]

[^68a-1]: Originally Art.68<sup>bis</sup>. Inserted by Art. 15 no 3 Final and Transitional Provisions to Titles XXIV-XXXIII CO ([AS 53 185](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/53/185_185_189); [BBl 1928 I 205](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1928/1_205_233_253), [1932 I 217](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1932/1_217__)). Amended by no II 3 of the FA of 5 Oct. 1984 on the Amendment of the Civil Code, in force since 1 Jan. 1988 ([AS 1986 122](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1986/122_122_122)[153](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1986/153_153_153)Art. 1; [BBl 1979 II 1191](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1979/2_1191_1179_1119)).
[^68a-2]: Repealed by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, with effect from 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### B. Special provisions

#### Art. 68b

[^68b-1]

<sup>1</sup> Either spouse may assert in the third-party claims procedure (Arts 106-109) that a distrained asset is the personal property of the debtor’s spouse.

<sup>2</sup> If the debt enforcement proceedings are limited to the debtor’s share of the common property in addition to his individual property, either spouse may also oppose the distraint of items of common property under the third-party claims procedure (Arts 106-109).

<sup>3</sup> If debt enforcement proceedings for satisfaction from individual property and the share in the common property are continued, distraint and realisation of the share in the common property are governed by Article 132; the right to distrain the future earned income of the spouse subject to debt enforcement remains reserved (Art. 93).[^68b-2]

<sup>4</sup> A spouse’s share of the common property may not be auctioned.

<sup>5</sup> The supervisory authority may request the judge to order the separation of property.

[^68b-1]: Inserted by no II 3 of the FA of 5 Oct. 1984 on the Amendment of the Civil Code, in force since 1 Jan. 1988 ([AS 1986 122](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1986/122_122_122)[153](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1986/153_153_153)Art. 1; [BBl 1979 II 1191](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1979/2_1191_1179_1119)).
[^68b-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### VII. Debt Enforcement Proceedings in the case of Legal Representation or Deputyship

[^tit2-lvlVII-1]

[^tit2-lvlVII-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 1. Minor debtor

#### Art. 68c

[^68c-1]

<sup>1</sup> If the debtor is a minor, the debt enforcement notices shall be served on the minor’s legal representative. In the event of the appointment of a child welfare advocate pursuant to Article 325 CC[^68c-2], the advocate and the holder of parental responsibility shall receive the debt enforcement notices, provided that the debt enforcement office has been notified of the appointment of the advocate.

<sup>2</sup> However, if the claim comes from an authorised business activity or is connected with the management of earnings or free assets by a minor (Arts 321 para. 2, 323 para. 1 and 327*b* CC), the debt enforcement notices shall be served on the debtor and the legal representative.

[^68c-1]: Amended by Annex No 12 of the FA of 19 Dec. 2008 (Adult Protection Law, Law of Persons and Law of Children), in force since 1 Jan. 2013 ([AS 2011 725](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2011/114); [BBl 2006 7001](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/899)).
[^68c-2]: [SR 210](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5703)

### 2. Adult debtor under an adult protection measure

#### Art. 68d

[^68d-1]

<sup>1</sup> If a deputy or a person entrusted with an adult’s care is responsible for managing an adult debtor’s assets and the adult protection authority has informed the debt enforcement office of this, the debt enforcement notices shall be served on the deputy or the person entrusted with the adult’s care.

<sup>2</sup> If the debtor’s capacity to act is not restricted, the debt enforcement notice shall also be served on the debtor.

[^68d-1]: Amended by Annex No 12 of the FA of 19 Dec. 2008 (Adult Protection Law, Law of Persons and Law of Children), in force since 1 Jan. 2013 ([AS 2011 725](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2011/114); [BBl 2006 7001](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/899)).

### 3. Limitation of liability

#### Art. 68e

If the debtor is only liable with his free assets, it may be claimed in the third-party claims procedure (Arts 106-109) that a distrained asset is not a part thereof.

### VIII. Summons for Payment and Filing an Objection to the Summons

[^tit2-lvlVIII-1]

[^tit2-lvlVIII-1]: Originally No VI.

### A. Summons for payment

### 1. Scope

#### Art. 69

<sup>1</sup> On receipt of the request for debt enforcement, the debt enforcement office shall issue a summons for payment.

<sup>2</sup>

The summons for payment contains:

- 1\. details of the request for debt enforcement;
- 2\. the request to satisfy the creditor for the claim together with the debt enforcement costs within 20 days or, if the debt enforcement proceedings are based on the provision of security, to provide security within this deadline;
- 3\. notification that if the debtor wishes to contest the claim or a part thereof or the right to assert it by way of debt enforcement, he must declare this to the debt enforcement office within ten days of service of the summons for payment (objection to the summons);
- 4\. the warning that if the debtor neither complies with the summons for payment nor files an objection to the summons, the debt enforcement proceedings will continue.

### 2. Official copies

#### Art. 70
