<sup>1</sup> The summons for payment is issued in duplicate. One copy is intended for the debtor, the other for the creditor. If the two documents are not identical, the copy delivered to the debtor shall take precedence.

<sup>2</sup> If joint debtors are sued at the same time, a separate summons for payment shall be served on each of them.[^70-1]

[^70-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 3. Time of service

#### Art. 71

<sup>1</sup> The summons for payment is served on the debtor after receipt of the request for debt enforcement.[^71-1]

<sup>2</sup> If there are several requests for debt enforcement against the same debtor, all the summons for payment must be served at the same time.

<sup>3</sup> In no case may a request received later be executed before an earlier request.

[^71-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 4. Form of service

#### Art. 72

<sup>1</sup> The summons for payment shall be served by the debt enforcement officer, an employee of the office or by post.[^72-1]

<sup>2</sup> On handing over the summons, the person effecting service must certify on both copies the date of service and the details of the person on whom the summons is served.

[^72-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### B. Inspection of the evidence

#### Art. 73

[^73-1]

<sup>1</sup> At any time after the debt enforcement proceedings begin, the debtor may demand that the creditor be requested to make evidence for his claim and a summary of all his claims against the debtor available for inspection at the debt enforcement office.

<sup>2</sup> This request shall have no effect on existing deadlines. If the creditor does not comply with the request or does not comply with it in time, the court shall take account of the fact that debtor was unable to inspect the evidence in its decision on procedural costs in any subsequent legal action.

[^73-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16. Dez. 2016, in force since 1 Jan. 2019 ([AS 2018 4583](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2018/721); [BBl 2015 3209](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2015/692)[5785](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2015/1322)).

### C. Objection

### 1. Deadline and form

#### Art. 74

<sup>1</sup> If the debtor wishes to object to the summons for payment, he must do so immediately verbally or in writing to the person serving the summons or within ten days of service to the debt enforcement office.[^74-1]

<sup>2</sup> If the debtor only partly disputes the claim, he must indicate the disputed amount precisely; if he fails to do this, the entire claim is regarded as disputed.[^74-2]

<sup>3</sup> If the debtor so requests, he must be issued free of charge with a certificate confirming his objection.

[^74-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^74-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Legal grounds

#### Art. 75

[^75-1]

<sup>1</sup> No legal grounds need be given for objecting to the summons for payment. Any person who nevertheless states such grounds is not precluded from stating other grounds at a later date.

<sup>2</sup> If the debtor disputes that he has acquired new assets (Arts 265, 265*a*), he must expressly declare this when objecting to the summons, failing which he may not raise this defence at a later date.

<sup>3</sup> The provisions on subsequent objection (Art. 77) and on filing an objection to enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange (Art. 179 para. 1) are reserved.

[^75-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 3. Notice to the creditor

#### Art. 76

<sup>1</sup> Notice of the grounds for filing an objection to the summons for payment shall be given to the creditor on his official copy of the summons for payment; if no objection to the summons has been filed, this must be indicated on the official copy.

<sup>2</sup> The official copy is sent to the creditor immediately after an objection to the summons is filed, or if no objection is filed, immediately after expiry of the deadline for objecting to the summons.

### 4. Subsequent objection in the case of a change in creditor

#### Art. 77

<sup>1</sup> If the creditor changes during the debt enforcement proceedings, the debtor may subsequently object to the summons for payment at any time prior to the distribution of the proceeds or declaration of bankruptcy.[^77-1]

<sup>2</sup> The debtor must file the objection in writing in the court at the place of debt enforcement within ten days of learning of the change in creditor, providing a statement of the grounds and entering credible defences against the new creditor.[^77-2]

<sup>3</sup> On receiving notice of the objection to the summons, the court may order the preliminary suspension of the debt enforcement proceedings; it decides on whether to admit the objection after hearing the parties.

<sup>4</sup> If subsequent objection is accepted, but distraint proceedings have already been carried out, the debt enforcement office shall allow the creditor ten days to request the recognition of his claim. If he does not file a request, the distraint proceedings become invalid.[^77-3]

<sup>5</sup> The debt enforcement office shall give the debtor notice of any change in creditor.[^77-4]

[^77-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^77-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^77-3]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^77-4]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 5. Effects

#### Art. 78

<sup>1</sup> The objection to the summons for payment has the effect of suspending the debt enforcement proceedings.

<sup>2</sup> If the debtor disputes only part of the claim, the debt enforcement proceedings may be continued in respect of the undisputed amount.

### D. Dismissal of the objection

### 1. In civil or administrative proceedings

#### Art. 79

[^79-1]

If the debtor files an objection to the summons for payment, the creditor must pursue his claim in civil or administrative proceedings. He may only secure the continuation of the debt enforcement proceedings on the basis of an enforceable decision that expressly dismisses the objection.

[^79-1]: Amended by Annex 1 No II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).

### 2. By setting aside the objection definitively

### a. Documents that entitle the objection to be set aside

#### Art. 80

[^80-1]

<sup>1</sup> If the claim is based on an enforceable court judgment, the creditor may request the court to dismiss the objection to the summons for payment (setting aside the objection definitively).[^80-2]

<sup>2</sup>

The following are equivalent to court judgments:[^80-3]

- 1\. court settlements and recognition of debts in court;
- 1<sup>bis</sup>[^80-4] enforceable public records under Articles 347–352 CPC[^80-5];
- 2\.[^80-6] orders issued by Swiss administrative authorities;
- 3\.[^80-7] …
- 4\.[^80-8] the final decisions of the inspectorates taken in application of Article 16 paragraph 1 of the Federal Act of 17 June 2005[^80-9] on Illegal Employment and which relate to inspection costs;
- 5\.[^80-10] in relation to value added tax: tax returns and assessment notices that have become legally binding by prescription, and assessment notices that have become legally binding through their written acceptance by the taxable person.

[^80-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^80-2]: Amended by Annex 1 No II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^80-3]: Amended by Annex 1 No II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^80-4]: Inserted by Annex 1 No II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^80-5]: [SR 272](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/6136)
[^80-6]: Amended by Annex 1 No II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^80-7]: Repealed by Annex 1 No II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, with effect from 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^80-8]: Inserted by Annex No 3 of the FA of 17 June 2005 on Illegal Employment, in force since 1 Jan. 2008 ([AS 2007 359](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2007/71); [BBl 2002 3605](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2002/520)).
[^80-9]: [SR 822.41](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/9926)
[^80-10]: Inserted by Annex No 2 of the FA of 30 Sept. 2016, in force since 1 Jan. 2018 ([AS 2017 3575](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/396); [BBl 2015 2615](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2015/540)).

### b. Defences

#### Art. 81

[^81-1]

<sup>1</sup> If the claim is based on an enforceable decision of a Swiss court or a Swiss administrative authority, the objection shall be set aside definitively unless the debtor provides documentary evidence that the debt has been repaid or deferred since the decision was issued or invokes the statute of limitations.

<sup>2</sup> If the claim is based on an enforceable public record, the debtor may assert further defences against the obligation to perform, provided they can be proven immediately.

<sup>3</sup> If a decision has been issued in another state, the debtor may also claim the defences provided for in the relevant international treaty or, in the absence of such a treaty, in the Federal Act of 18 December 1987[^81-2] on Private International Law, unless a Swiss court has already ruled on these defences.[^81-3]

[^81-1]: Amended by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^81-2]: [SR 291](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/6186)
[^81-3]: Amended by Art. 3 no 2 of the FedD of 11 Dec. 2009 (Approval and Implementation of the Lugano Convention), in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 5601](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/800); [BBl 2009 1777](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2009/402)).

### 3. By setting aside the objection provisionally

### a. Requirements

#### Art. 82

<sup>1</sup> If the claim is based on recognition of the claim evidenced by a public deed or confirmed by signature, the creditor may request that the objection be set aside provisionally.

<sup>2</sup> The court shall grant the same provided the debtor does not immediately enter credible defences that rebut the recognition of the claim.

### b. Effects

#### Art. 83

<sup>1</sup> A creditor who has been successful in having the objection set aside provisionally may demand provisional distraint proceedings on expiry of the payment deadline, depending on the person of the debtor, or that an inventory be made in accordance with Article 162.

<sup>2</sup> The debtor may however within 20 days of the objection being set aside bring an action to have the claim declared unfounded under ordinary procedure in the court at the place of debt enforcement.[^83-1]

<sup>3</sup> If the debtor fails to do this or if the action to have the debt declared unfounded is dismissed, the provisional decision to set aside the objection and if applicable the provisional distraint proceedings shall become definitive.[^83-2]

<sup>4</sup> The deadline under Article 165 paragraph 2 shall be suspended between the initiation of and the decision on the action being raised to have the debt declared unfounded. However, the bankruptcy court shall annul the effects of the inventory if the requirements for ordering it are no longer met.[^83-3]

[^83-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^83-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^83-3]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 4. Procedure for setting aside the objection

#### Art. 84

[^84-1]

<sup>1</sup> The court at the place of debt enforcement shall decide on applications to set aside the objection.

<sup>2</sup> Immediately on receipt of the application, it shall give the debtor the opportunity to respond orally or in writing and shall then give notice of its decision within five days.

[^84-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### E. Judicial dismissal or suspension of the debt enforcement proceedings

### 1. In summary proceedings

#### Art. 85

[^85-1]

If the debtor proves by official documents that the debt together with interest and costs has been settled or deferred, he may at any time request the court at the place of debt enforcement in the former case to dismiss, and in the latter case to suspend the debt enforcement proceedings.

[^85-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. In ordinary or simplified proceedings

[^tit2-lvlVIII-lvlD-lvl2-1]

[^tit2-lvlVIII-lvlD-lvl2-1]: Amended by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).

#### Art. 85a

[^85a-1]

<sup>1</sup> Irrespective of whether an objection to the summons for payment is filed, the debtor may at any time have the court at the place of debt enforcement declare that the debt does not exist or no longer exists or has been deferred. [^85a-2]

<sup>2</sup>

On the action being filed, the court shall hear the parties and assess the evidence; if the court considers the action to be very probably well-founded, it shall provisionally suspend debt enforcement proceedings:

- 1\. in debt enforcement proceedings by distraint or debt enforcement proceedings to realise a pledge prior to realisation or, if this has already taken place, prior to distribution;
- 2\. in bankruptcy proceedings after service of the notice of impending bankruptcy.

<sup>3</sup> If the court upholds the action, it shall cancel or discontinue the debt enforcement proceedings.

<sup>4</sup> …[^85a-3]

[^85a-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^85a-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 2016, in force since 1 Jan. 2019 ([AS 2018 4583](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2018/721); [BBl 2015 3209](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2015/692)[5785](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2015/1322)).
[^85a-3]: Repealed by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, with effect from 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).

### F. Action for restitution of money paid without legal cause

#### Art. 86

<sup>1</sup> If no objection to the summons for payment was filed or if the objection was set aside, the person who has paid an undue debt as a result may recover the amount paid through legal proceedings within one year of the payment.[^86-1]

<sup>2</sup> The plaintiff has the choice of bringing the action for restitution to court either at the place of debt enforcement or at the place where the defendant has his ordinary place of jurisdiction.

<sup>3</sup> In derogation from Article 63 of the Swiss Code of Obligations (CO)[^86-2], this right of restitution is not dependent on any condition other than proof that the debt was not due.[^86-3]

[^86-1]: Amended by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, in force since 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).
[^86-2]: [SR 220](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5818)
[^86-3]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### G. Debt enforcement proceedings to realise a pledge and debt enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange

#### Art. 87

The special provisions of Articles 151-153 apply to the summons for payment in debt enforcement proceedings to realise a pledge, and those of Articles 178-189 apply to the summons for payment and filing an objection to debt enforcement proceedings based on a bill of exchange.

### IX. Continuation of Debt Enforcement

[^tit2-lvlIX-1]

[^tit2-lvlIX-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

#### Art. 88

[^88-1]

<sup>1</sup> If the debt enforcement proceedings have not been suspended by an objection to the summons for payment or by court decision, the creditor may file a request for continuation no earlier than 20 days after service of the summons.

<sup>2</sup> This right expires one year after service of the summons. If an objection to the summons has been filed, this period shall be suspended between the initiation and settlement of any court or administrative proceedings brought as a result.

<sup>3</sup> Receipt of the request for continuation shall be certified to the creditor free of charge on request.

<sup>4</sup> A claim in a foreign currency may, at the creditor's request, be converted back into the national currency at the exchange rate on the date of the request for continuation.

[^88-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

## Title Three Debt Enforcement by Distraint

[^tit3-1]

[^tit3-1]: Originally before Art. 88.

### I. Distraint

[^tit3-lvlI-1]

[^tit3-lvlI-1]: Originally before Art. 88.

### A. Execution

### 1. Time

#### Art. 89

[^89-1]

Where the debtor is subject to debt enforcement by distraint, the debt enforcement office, on receipt of the application for continuation, must immediately distrain the assets or have them distrained by the debt enforcement office at the place where the assets to be distrained are located.

[^89-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Notice

#### Art. 90

The debtor shall be notified of the distraint proceedings on the preceding day at the latest, with reference being made to Article 91.

### 3. Duties of the debtor and third parties

#### Art. 91

[^91-1]

<sup>1</sup>

The debtor is obliged, subject to criminal penalties for failing to do so:

- 1\. to attend the distraint proceedings or be represented during the same (Art. 323 para. 1 SCC[^91-2]);
- 2\. to disclose his assets, including those not in his custody, as well as his claims and rights against third parties, to the extent that this is necessary for sufficient distraint (Arts 163 no 1 and 323 no 2 SCC)[^91-3].

<sup>2</sup> If the debtor fails to attend the distraint without sufficient excuse and does not allow himself to be represented, the debt enforcement office may have him brought to the proceedings by the police.

<sup>3</sup> The debtor must open premises and containers to the officer on request. The official may use police powers if necessary.

<sup>4</sup> Third parties who hold the debtor’s assets in safekeeping or with whom the debtor has assets are obliged to provide information to the same extent as the debtor, subject to criminal penalties for failing to do so (Art. 324 para. 5 SCC).

<sup>5</sup> Authorities are obliged to provide information to the same extent as the debtor.

<sup>6</sup> The debt enforcement office shall expressly draw the attention of the persons concerned to their obligations and to the penalties for failing to meet the same.

[^91-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^91-2]: [SR 311.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/6196)
[^91-3]: [AS 2005 79](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2005/15)

### 4. Undistrainable assets

#### Art. 92

<sup>1</sup>

The following are exempt from distraint:

- 1\.[^92-1] items for the personal use of the debtor and his family, such as clothing, personal effects, household appliances, furniture or other movable items, insofar as they are indispensable;
- 1*a*.[^92-2] animals kept for domestic purposes and not for property or profit;
- 2\.[^92-3] books of religious edification and items of worship;
- 3\.[^92-4] tools, equipment, instruments and books, insofar as they are necessary for the debtor and his family to practise their profession;
- 4\.[^92-5] at the choice of the debtor, either two dairy cows or other cattle, or four goats or sheep, as well as small animals, together with the fodder and straw necessary for their subsistence and bedding for four months, provided the animals are indispensable to the debtor and his family or for the maintenance of his business;
- 5\.[^92-6] the food and combustible fuels required by the debtor and his family for the two months following the distraint proceedings or the cash or claims required to purchase them;
- 6\.[^92-7] the clothing, equipment and weapons, the service horse and the pay of a member of the armed forces, the pocket money of a person performing civilian service as well as the clothing and equipment and allowance of a person performing civil protection service;
- 7\.[^92-8] the underlying right to the life annuities created in accordance with Articles 516˗520 CO[^92-9];
- 8\.[^92-10] welfare benefits and support from relief, health and welfare funds, death benefit organisations and similar institutions;
- 9\.[^92-11] pensions, lump-sum settlements and other benefits paid to victims or their relatives for bodily injury, health disorders or the death of a person, insofar as such benefits constitute satisfaction, compensation for medical expenses or for the purchase of aids;
- 9*a*.[^92-12] pensions pursuant to Article 20 of the Federal Act of 20 December 1946[^92-13] on Old Age and Survivors’ Insurance or pursuant to Article 50 of the Federal Act of 19 June 1959[^92-14] on Invalidity Insurance, benefits pursuant to Article 12 of the Federal Act of 19 March 1965[^92-15] on Benefits Supplementary to the Old Age, Survivors’ and Disability Insurance and benefits from family compensation funds;
- 10\.[^92-16] claims to pension and vested benefits against an occupational pension scheme before they fall due;
- 11\.[^92-17] assets of a foreign state or a foreign central bank that serve sovereign purposes.

<sup>2</sup> Items for which it can be assumed from the outset that the surplus of the realisation proceeds over the costs would be so small that removal is not justified may not be distrained. However, they must be noted in the deed of distraint together with the estimated amount.[^92-18]

<sup>3</sup> High-value items in accordance with paragraph 1, numbers 1-3 are distrainable; However, they may only be taken away from the debtor if the creditor provides replacement items of the same utility value or the amount required to purchase them before they are taken away.[^92-19]

<sup>4</sup> The special provisions on undistrainability of the Federal Act of 2 April 1908[^92-20] on Insurance Policies (Arts 79 para. 2 and 80 IPA), the Copyright Act of 9 October 1992[^92-21] (Art. 18 CopA) and the Swiss Criminal Code[^92-22] (Art. 378 para. 2 SCC) remain reserved.[^92-23]

[^92-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^92-2]: Inserted by no IV of the FA of 4 Oct. 2002 (Article on Animals), in force since 1 April 2003 ([AS 2003 463](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2003/100); [BBl 2002 4164](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2002/591)[5806](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2002/895)).

[^92-12]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 5. Limits on distrainable income

#### Art. 93

[^93-1]

<sup>1</sup> Earned income in any form, usufructs and their revenues, life annuities, and maintenance payments, pensions and payments in any form that compensate for a loss of income or a right to maintenance, in particular pensions and lump-sum payments that are not exempted from distraint under Article 92, may be distrained unless the debt enforcement officer is of the view that they are absolutely essential to the debtor and his family.

<sup>2</sup> Such income may be distrained for one year at the most; this period begins with the execution of distraint. If two or more creditors participate in the distraint proceedings, the period runs from the first distraint that is executed at the request of a creditor of the respective group (Arts 110 and 111).

<sup>3</sup> If, during the period of such distraint, it comes to the attention of the office that the circumstances that are decisive in determining the distrainable amount have changed, the office shall modify the distraint proceedings to take account of the new circumstances.

<sup>4</sup> At the request of the debtor, the office shall instruct the debtor’s employer to transfer to the office, in addition, the amount required to pay the current premium and share of cost debts due to the compulsory health insurer for the duration of the distraint of earnings, insofar as these premiums and share of cost debts are part of the debtor’s subsistence minimum. The office shall use this to settle the current premium and share of cost debts directly with the insurer.[^93-2]

[^93-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^93-2]: Inserted by No III of the FA of 18 March 2022 (Enforcing the Obligation to Pay Premiums), in force since 1 July 2024 ([AS 2023 678](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/678); [BBl 2021 745](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2021/745), [1058](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2021/1058)).

### 6. Distraint of crops before harvest

#### Art. 94

<sup>1</sup>

Hanging and standing crops may not be distrained:

- 1\. in the meadows before 1 April;
- 2\. in the fields before 1 June;
- 3\. in the vineyards before 20 August.

<sup>2</sup> Any sale before or on the specified dates of harvested crops is invalid as against the distraining creditor.

<sup>3</sup> The rights of a mortgagee to hanging and standing crops as part of the pledged property are reserved, but only on condition that the mortgagee himself has initiated enforcement proceedings for realisation of the mortgage[^94-1] before the realisation of the pledged crops takes place.[^94-2]

[^94-1]: Term pursuant to No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^94-2]: Amended by Art. 58 Final Title CC, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title CC; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).

### 7. Order of distraint

### a. In general

#### Art. 95

<sup>1</sup> In the first instance, movable assets, including claims and claims subject to limited distrainability (Art. 93), shall be attached. The first items to be distrained are everyday items; However, more dispensable assets shall be distrained before those less dispensable.[^95-1]

<sup>2</sup> Immovable assets shall only be distrained if the movable assets are insufficient to cover the debt.[^95-2]

<sup>3</sup> In the last instance, assets which have been attached or which are designated by the debtor as belonging to third parties or are claimed by third parties shall be distrained.

<sup>4</sup> If fodder stocks are distrained, a corresponding number of livestock must also be distrained at the debtor’s request.

<sup>4bis</sup> The officer may derogate from this order if the circumstances justify it or if the creditor and debtor jointly request so.[^95-3]

<sup>5</sup> Moreover, the officer shall, as far as practicable, take into account the interests of both the creditor and the debtor.

[^95-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^95-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^95-3]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### b. Claims against a spouse or registered partner

#### Art. 95a

[^95a-1]

Claims of the debtor against his or her spouse or registered partner shall only be distrained if his or her other assets are insufficient.

[^95a-1]: Inserted by No II 3 of the FA of 5 Oct. 1984 on the Amendment of the Civil Code ([AS 1986 122](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1986/122_122_122); [BBl 1979 II 1191](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1979/2_1191_1179_1119)). Last amended by Annex No 16 of the Same-Sex Partnership Act of 18 June 2004, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2005 5685](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2005/782); [BBl 2003 1288](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2003/194)).

### B. Effects of distraint

#### Art. 96

<sup>1</sup> The debtor may not dispose of the distrained assets without the authorisation of the debt enforcement officer, subject to criminal penalties for doing so (Art. 169 SCC[^96-1]). The distraining officer shall expressly draw his attention to this and to the penalty.[^96-2]

<sup>2</sup> Disposals by the debtor are invalid insofar as they infringe the rights of the creditors arising from distraint, subject to the consequences of third parties acquiring possession in good faith.[^96-3]

[^96-1]: [SR 311.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/6196)
[^96-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^96-3]: Inserted by Art. 58 Final Title CC, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title CC; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).

### C. Estimated value. Extent of distraint

#### Art. 97

<sup>1</sup> The officer shall estimate the value of the distrained items, if necessary, with the assistance of experts.

<sup>2</sup> No more shall be distrained than is necessary to satisfy the distraining creditors’ claims, including interest and costs.

### D. Protective measures

### 1. In the case of movable property

#### Art. 98

<sup>1</sup> Money, banknotes, bearer instruments, bills of exchange and other endorsable papers, precious metals and other valuables shall be held in safekeeping by the debt enforcement office.[^98-1]

<sup>2</sup> Other movable items may be left temporarily in the possession of the debtor or a third-party holder against the undertaking to keep them available at all times.

<sup>3</sup> However, these items must also be taken into official safekeeping or handed over to a third party for safekeeping if the debt enforcement officer deems it appropriate or the creditor can credibly demonstrate that this is necessary to secure his rights established by distraint.[^98-2]

<sup>4</sup> The debt enforcement office may also take possession if a third party has a pledge on the property. If it is not realised, it shall be returned to the pledgee.

[^98-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^98-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 3 April 1924, in force since 1 Jan. 1925 (AS **40** 391; [BBl 1921 I 507](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1921/1_507_579_)).

### 2. In the case of claims

#### Art. 99

When distraining claims or other rights for which there is no document made out to the holder or transferable by endorsement, a debtor of the debtor subject to enforcement shall be informed that he may only make legally valid payments to the debt enforcement office.

### 3. In the case of other rights, debt collection

#### Art. 100

The debt enforcement office shall ensure that the distrained rights are maintained and shall collect payment for debts due.

### 4. In the case of immovable property

### a. Priority notice in the land register

#### Art. 101

[^101-1]

<sup>1</sup> The distraint of immovable property has the effect of a restriction on the power of disposal. The debt enforcement office shall notify the land register office without delay, stating the date and the amount for which distraint was made, for the purpose of entering the priority notice. Notice must also be given of the participation of new creditors in the distraint proceedings and the cancellation of distraint.

<sup>2</sup> The priority notice shall be cancelled if the application for realisation is not filed within two years of distraint.

[^101-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### b. Crops and yields

#### Art. 102

[^102-1]

<sup>1</sup> Subject to the rights of the mortgagees, the distraint of immovable property also covers its crops and other yields.

<sup>2</sup> The debt enforcement office must inform the mortgagees and, where applicable, the tenants or lessees of the distraint proceedings.

<sup>3</sup> It is responsible for the administration and management of the immovable property[^102-2].

[^102-1]: Amended by Art. 58 Final Title CC, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title CC; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).
[^102-2]: Term pursuant to No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)). This amendment has been made throughout the text.

### c. Harvesting the crop

#### Art. 103

<sup>1</sup> The debt enforcement office shall ensure that the crops are harvested (Arts 94 and 102).[^103-1]

<sup>2</sup> If necessary, the crop shall be used for the subsistence of the debtor and his family.

[^103-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 5. In the case of communal rights

#### Art. 104

If a usufruct or a share in an undistributed estate, joint property or other joint assets is distrained, the debt enforcement office shall notify the third parties involved of the distraint proceedings.

### 6. Costs of storage and maintenance

#### Art. 105

[^105-1]

On request, the creditor shall advance the costs of storage and maintenance of distrained assets to the debt enforcement office.

[^105-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### E. Third-party claims procedure

### 1. Noting and notice

#### Art. 106

[^106-1]

<sup>1</sup> If it is asserted that a third party has the ownership of, a pledge on or another right to the distrained item that prevents distraint or that must be taken into account in the further course of the enforcement proceedings, the debt enforcement office shall note the third party’s claim in the deed of distraint or, if the deed has already been served, notify the parties separately.

<sup>2</sup> Third parties may file their claims as long as the proceeds from the realisation of the distrained item have not yet been distributed.

<sup>3</sup> After realisation, the third party may assert the claims to which it is entitled under civil law in the event of theft, loss or other misappropriation of a movable item (Arts 934 and 935 CC[^106-2]) or in the event of bad faith on the part of the acquirer (Arts 936 and 974 para. 3 CC) outside the debt enforcement proceedings. A public auction within the meaning of Article 934 paragraph 2 CC shall also be deemed to be a private sale in accordance with Article 130 of this Act.

[^106-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^106-2]: [SR 210](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5703)

### 2. Enforcement

### a. In the case of debtor’s exclusive custody

#### Art. 107

[^107-1]

<sup>1</sup> The debtor and creditor may contest the third party’s claim at the debt enforcement office if the claim relates to

- 1\. a movable item in the exclusive custody of the debtor;
- 2\. a claim or other right, provided that the debtor’s entitlement is more likely than that of the third party;
- 3\. immovable property, unless the claim is evidenced by the land register.

<sup>2</sup> The debt enforcement office shall set them a deadline of ten days to do so.

<sup>3</sup> On the demand of the debtor or the creditor, the third party shall be requested to submit his evidence to the debt enforcement office for inspection within the time limit for contesting the claim. Article 73 paragraph 2 applies *mutatis mutandis*.

<sup>4</sup> If the third party's claim is not contested, it shall be deemed to have been recognised in the debt enforcement proceedings in question.

<sup>5</sup> If the claim is contested, the debt enforcement office shall set the third party a time limit of 20 days within which it may bring an action against the disputing party to establish its claim. If he does not file an action, the claim shall not be considered in the debt enforcement proceedings in question.

[^107-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### b. In the case of the third party’s custody or co-custody

#### Art. 108

[^108-1]

<sup>1</sup> The creditor and debtor may bring an action against the third party to have his claim declared unfounded if the claim relates to

- 1\. a movable item in the custody or joint custody of the third party;
- 2\. a claim or other right, provided that the third party’s entitlement is more likely than that of the debtor;
- 3 immovable property, provided the claim is recorded in the land register.

<sup>2</sup> The debt enforcement office shall set them a deadline of 20 days to do so.

<sup>3</sup> If no action is filed, the claim shall be deemed to have been recognised in the debt enforcement proceedings in question.

<sup>4</sup> On the demand of the creditor or the debtor, the third party shall be requested to submit his evidence to the debt enforcement office for inspection within the time limit for bringing an action. Article 73 paragraph 2 applies *mutatis mutandis*.

[^108-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### c. Place of jurisdiction

#### Art. 109

[^109-1]

<sup>1</sup>

The following must be filed in the court at the place of debt enforcement:

- 1\. actions under Article 107 paragraph 5;
- 2\. actions under Article 108 paragraph 1 if the defendant is domiciled abroad.

<sup>2</sup> If the action under Article 108 paragraph 1 is brought against a defendant domiciled in Switzerland, it must be filed in the court of the defendant's domicile.

<sup>3</sup> If the claim relates to immovable property, the action must in every case be filed in the court of the place where the property or its most valuable part is located.

<sup>4</sup> The court shall notify the debt enforcement office of the receipt and conclusion of the action. …[^109-2]

<sup>5</sup> Until the action has been concluded, the debt enforcement proceedings in respect of the disputed items shall be suspended and the time limits for requests for realisation (Art. 116) shall be suspended.

[^109-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^109-2]: Second sentence repealed by Annex 1 no II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, with effect from 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).

### F. Participation in distraint proceedings

### 1. In general

#### Art. 110

[^110-1]

<sup>1</sup> Creditors who submit a request for continuation within 30 days of the execution of distraint shall participate in the distraint proceedings. The distraint proceedings shall be extended in each case to the extent necessary to cover all the claims of the creditor group.

<sup>2</sup> Creditors who only submit the request for continuation after the 30-day period has expired shall in the same way form additional groups with separate distraint proceedings.

<sup>3</sup> Assets that have already been distrained may be distrained again, but only to the extent that their proceeds are not to be paid to the creditors for whom the previous distraint proceedings were carried out.

[^110-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Privileged participation

#### Art. 111

[^111-1]

<sup>1</sup>

The following persons may participate in the distraint proceedings within 40 days of their execution without bringing debt enforcement proceedings beforehand:

- 1\.[^111-2] the debtor’s spouse or registered partner;
- 2\.[^111-3] the debtor’s children for claims arising from the parental relationship and adult persons for claims arising from an advance care directive (Arts 360–369 CC[^111-4]);
- 3\.[^111-5] the debtor’s adult children and grandchildren for claims arising from Articles 334 and 334<sup>bis</sup> CC;
- 4\. the debtor’s beneficiary under a lifetime maintenance agreement for his claim for compensation under Article 529 CO[^111-6].

<sup>2</sup> The persons mentioned in paragraph 1 numbers 1 and 2 may only claim their rights if the distraint proceedings are carried out during or within one year of the end of the marriage, the registered partnership, the parental relationship or the period in which the advance care directives is in effect; the duration of litigation or debt enforcement proceedings is not included in the calculation. The child and adult protection authority may also file a declaration of intention to participate on behalf of the children or of a person subject to an adult protection measure.[^111-7]

<sup>3</sup> Where the debt enforcement office is aware of persons entitled to participate, it shall notify these persons of the distraint proceedings by standard post.

<sup>4</sup> The debt enforcement office shall notify the debtor and the creditors of any such claim that is made and allow them ten days within which to dispute the claim.

<sup>5</sup> If the claim is contested, the claimant may only participate with the rights under provisional distraint proceedings, and the claimant must bring an action within 20 days in the court at the place of debt enforcement; if he fails to meet this deadline, his right to participate becomes invalid. …[^111-8]

[^111-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^111-2]: Amended by Annex No 16 of the Same-Sex Partnership Act of 18 June 2004, in force since 1 Jan. 2007 ([AS 2005 5685](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2005/782); [BBl 2003 1288](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2003/194)).
[^111-3]: Amended by Annex No 12 of the FA of 19 Dec. 2008 (Adult Protection, Law of Persons and of Children), in force since 1 Jan. 2013 ([AS 2011 725](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2011/114); [BBl 2006 7001](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/899)).
[^111-4]: [SR 210](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5703)
[^111-5]: Amended by Annex No 12 of the FA of 19 Dec. 2008 (Adult Protection, Law of Persons and of Children), in force since 1 Jan. 2013 ([AS 2011 725](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2011/114); [BBl 2006 7001](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/899)).
[^111-6]: [SR 220](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5818)
[^111-7]: Amended by Annex No 12 of the FA of 19 Dec. 2008 (Adult Protection, Law of Persons and of Children), in force since 1 Jan. 2013 ([AS 2011 725](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2011/114); [BBl 2006 7001](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/899)).
[^111-8]: Second sentence repealed by Annex 1 No II 17 of the Civil Procedure Code of 19 Dec. 2008, with effect from 1 Jan. 2011 ([AS 2010 1739](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2010/262); [BBl 2006 7221](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2006/914)).

### G. Deed of distraint

### 1. Recording

#### Art. 112

<sup>1</sup> In any distraint proceedings, a document (the deed of distraint) signed by the officer or employee carrying out the proceedings must be recorded. The document shall list the creditor and the debtor, the amount of the claim, the date and time of distraint, the distrained assets and their estimated value and, if applicable, the claims of third parties.

<sup>2</sup> If goods are distrained that are already subject to an attachment, the participation of the attachment creditor in the distraint proceedings (Art. 281) shall be noted.

<sup>3</sup> If no distrainable assets are available or the assets are insufficient, this fact shall be noted in the deed of distraint.

### 2. Subsequent additions

#### Art. 113

[^113-1]

If new creditors take part in distraint proceedings or if the distraint proceedings are expanded, this shall be added subsequently to the deed of distraint.

[^113-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 3. Service on creditors and debtor

#### Art. 114

[^114-1]

The debt enforcement office shall serve the creditors and the debtor with a copy of the deed of distraint immediately on expiry of the 30-day deadline for participation.

[^114-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 4. Deed of distraint as an unpaid debt certificate

#### Art. 115

<sup>1</sup> If no distrainable assets were available, the deed of distraint serves as the unpaid debt certificate in terms of Article 149.

<sup>2</sup> If insufficient assets were available in the estimation of the officer, the deed of distraint shall serve the creditor as a provisional unpaid debt certificate and as such has the legal effects mentioned in Articles 271 number 5 and 285.

<sup>3</sup> The provisional unpaid debt certificate also confers on the creditor the right to request the distraint of assets newly discovered within the one-year deadline under Article 88 paragraph 2. The provisions on participation in distraint proceedings (Arts 110 and 111) apply.[^115-1]

[^115-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### II. Realisation

### A. Request for realisation

### 1. Deadlines

#### Art. 116

[^116-1]

<sup>1</sup> The creditors may request the realisation of distrained movable assets, claims and other rights no earlier than one month and at the latest one year after distraint, and the realisation of distrained immovable property no earlier than six months and at the latest two years after distraint.

<sup>2</sup> Where a future salary has been distrained, and if the employer has not paid over the distrained amounts by the date on which they were due for payment, the realisation of the claim to these amounts may be requested within 15 months after distraint.

<sup>3</sup> Where the distraint proceedings have been extended because of the participation of several creditors, these deadlines shall run from the last successful additional distraint procedure.

[^116-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Entitlement

#### Art. 117

<sup>1</sup> Each individual participant in a group of creditors has the right to request realisation.

<sup>2</sup> Creditors who have distrained assets in accordance with Article 110 paragraph 3 only for the additional proceeds may also request their realisation.

### 3. In provisional distraint proceedings

#### Art. 118

Creditors whose distraint proceedings are merely provisional may not request realisation. During the provisional period, he deadlines in Article 116 do not apply to such creditors.

### 4. Effects

#### Art. 119

[^119-1]

<sup>1</sup> The distrained assets shall be realised in accordance with Articles 122–143*a*.

<sup>2</sup> Realisation is suspended as soon as the proceeds reach the total amount of the claims for which distraint is provisional or definitive. Article 144 paragraph 5 is reserved.

[^119-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 5. Notification of the debtor

#### Art. 120

The debt enforcement office shall notify the debtor within three days of the request for realisation.

### 6. Lapse of the debt enforcement proceedings

#### Art. 121

If the request for realisation is not made within the statutory deadline or is withdrawn and not filed again, the debt enforcement proceedings shall lapse.

### B. Realisation of movable items and claims

### 1. Deadlines

### a. General

#### Art. 122

<sup>1</sup> The debt enforcement office shall realise movable items and claims no earlier than ten days and at the latest two months from receipt of the request.[^122-1]

<sup>2</sup> The realisation of hanging or standing crops may not take place before the crops is ready for harvest without the consent of the debtor.

[^122-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### b. Deferral of realisation

#### Art. 123

[^123-1]

<sup>1</sup> If the debtor can credibly demonstrate that he can repay the debt in instalments and undertakes to make regular and reasonable payments on account to the debt enforcement office, the debt enforcement officer may defer realisation for a maximum of twelve months from receipt of the first instalment.[^123-2]

<sup>2</sup> In the case of debt enforcement proceedings for first-class claims (Art. 219 para. 4), realisation may be deferred for a maximum of six months.[^123-3]

<sup>3</sup> The debt enforcement officer shall determine the amount and due dates of the instalment payments; he must take into account the circumstances of both the debtor and the creditor.

<sup>4</sup> The deferral shall be extended by the duration of any stay of enforcement. In this case, the instalments and their due date will be redetermined after the expiry of the stay of enforcement.[^123-4]

<sup>5</sup> The debt enforcement officer shall amend his order *ex officio* or at the request of the creditor or the debtor if the circumstances so require. The deferral shall be cancelled automatically if an instalment payment is not made on time.[^123-5]

[^123-1]: Amended by Art. 5 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).
[^123-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^123-3]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^123-4]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^123-5]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### c. Early realisation

#### Art. 124

<sup>1</sup> At the debtor’s request, realisation[^124-1] may take place even if the creditor is not yet entitled to demand it.

<sup>2</sup> The debt enforcement officer may at any time realise objects that are subject to rapid depreciation, require costly maintenance or cost a disproportionately high amount to store.[^124-2]

[^124-1]: Term pursuant to No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)). This amendment has been made throughout the text.
[^124-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Auction

### a. Preparation

#### Art. 125

<sup>1</sup> Realisation shall be effected by way of public auction. The place, day and hour of the same shall be publicly announced in advance.

<sup>2</sup> The form of announcement and the manner, place and date of the auction shall be determined by the debt enforcement officer in such a way that the interests of the parties involved are taken into account as far as possible. Announcement in the Official Gazette is not required in this case.

<sup>3</sup> If the debtor, the creditor and the third parties involved have a known place of residence or a representative in Switzerland, the debt enforcement office shall inform them of the time and place of the auction by standard post at least three days before the auction.[^125-1]

[^125-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### b. Sale, Coverage principle

#### Art. 126

[^126-1]

<sup>1</sup> The item to be realised shall be sold to the highest bidder after three calls, provided that the bid exceeds the amount of any claims secured by lien ranking prior to the creditor seeking enforcement.

<sup>2</sup> If no such bid is made, the debt enforcement proceedings in respect of this item shall lapse.

[^126-1]: Amended by Art. 6 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).

### c. Dispensing with realisation

#### Art. 127

[^127-1]

If it can be assumed from the outset that a sale in accordance with Article 126 will not be possible, the debt enforcement officer may, at the request of the creditor seeking enforcement, dispense with realising the claim and issue an unpaid debt certificate.

[^127-1]: Amended by Art. 6 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).

### d. Precious metal items

#### Art. 128

[^128-1]

Items made of precious metal may not be sold for less than their metal value.

[^128-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### e. Mode of payment and consequences of default on payment

#### Art. 129

<sup>1</sup> Payment must be made immediately after the sale. However, the debt enforcement officer may allow a period of a maximum of 20 days for payment to be made. The item shall only be handed over when the payment is irrevocably in the possession of the debt enforcement office.[^129-1]

<sup>2</sup> Payment may be made in cash up to an amount of CHF 100,000. If the price is higher, the portion exceeding this amount must be paid through a financial intermediary in accordance with the Anti-Money Laundering Act of 10 October 1997[^129-2]. The debt enforcement officer shall determine the other terms of payment.[^129-3]

<sup>3</sup> If payment is not made on time, the debt enforcement office must order a new auction, to which Article 126 applies.[^129-4]

<sup>4</sup> The former successful bidder and his guarantors shall be liable for the loss and any further damage. Interest shall be charged on the loss at five per cent.

[^129-1]: Amended by No I 3 of the FA of 12 Dec. 2014 on the Implementation of the 2012 Revised Recommendations of the Financial Action Task Force, in force since 1 Jan. 2016 ([AS 2015 1389](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2015/289); [BBl 2014 605](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2014/100)).
[^129-2]: [SR 955.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/11599)
[^129-3]: Amended by No I 3 of the FA of 12 Dec. 2014 on the Implementation of the 2012 Revised Recommendations of the Financial Action Task Force, in force since 1 Jan. 2016 ([AS 2015 1389](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2015/289); [BBl 2014 605](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2014/100)).
[^129-4]: Amended by Art. 7 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).

### 3. Sale by private contract

#### Art. 130

A sale by private contract may be held instead of an auction:[^130-1]

- 1\.[^130-2] if all parties involved expressly agree;
- 2\. if securities or other items with a market or stock exchange price are to be realised[^130-3] and the price offered is equal to the current market price;
- 3\.[^130-4] if this price is offered for items made of precious metal for which the bids at the auction did not reach the metal value;
- 4\. in the case of Article 124 paragraph 2.

[^130-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^130-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^130-3]: Term pursuant to No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)). This amendment has been made throughout the text.
[^130-4]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 4. Transfer of claims

#### Art. 131

<sup>1</sup> Monetary claims of the debtor which have no market or stock exchange price shall, if all distraining creditors so request, be assigned either to all the creditors or to individual creditors for joint account at nominal value in lieu of payment. In this case, the creditors shall assume the rights of the debtor subject to debt enforcement up to the amount of their claims.

<sup>2</sup> If all distraining creditors agree, they or individual creditors may assert distrained claims in their own name and for their own account and risk, without prejudice to their rights as against the debtor subject to debt enforcement. They shall require authorisation from the debt enforcement office to do so. The result serves to cover the expenses and claims of those creditors who have proceeded in this way. Any surplus must be paid to the debt enforcement office.[^131-1]

[^131-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 5. Special realisation procedure

#### Art. 132

[^132-1]

<sup>1</sup> If assets of a different nature are to be realised, such as a usufruct or a share in an undistributed estate, ownership in undivided shares, in partnership property or in another form of communal property, the debt enforcement officer shall request the supervisory authority to determine the procedure.

<sup>2</sup> The same rule applies to the realisation of inventions, plant variety rights, industrial designs and models, trademarks and copyrights.[^132-2]

<sup>3</sup> The supervisory authority may, after hearing the parties involved, order the auction, delegate realisation to an administrator or make other arrangements.

[^132-1]: Amended by Art. 8 of the FA of 28 Sept. 1949, in force since 1 Feb. 1950 (AS **1950** I 57; [BBl 1948 I 1218](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1948/1_1218_1201_)).
[^132-2]: Amended by Art. 52 No I of the Plant Variety Protection Act of 20 March 1975, in force since 1 June 1977 ([AS 1977 862](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1977/862_862_862); [BBl 1974 I 1469](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1974/1_1469_1409_1399)).

### 6. Contesting realisation

#### Art. 132a

[^132a-1]

<sup>1</sup> Realisation may only be contested by means of an appeal against the auction sale or the sale by private contract.

<sup>2</sup> The period allowed to file the appeal under Article 17 paragraph 2 begins when the appellant has become aware of the contested act of realisation and the grounds for contesting the same have become apparent to him.

<sup>3</sup> The right of appeal expires one year after realisation.

[^132a-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### C. Realisation of immovable property

### 1. Deadline

#### Art. 133

[^133-1]

<sup>1</sup> Properties shall be sold by public auction by the debt enforcement office no earlier than one month and no later than three months after receipt of the request for realisation.

<sup>2</sup> At the request of the debtor and with the express consent of all distraining and mortgagees, realisation may take place even if no creditor is yet entitled to demand it.

[^133-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 2. Auction terms

### a. Availability for inspection

#### Art. 134

<sup>1</sup> The debt enforcement office shall draw up the auction terms in the manner customary at the location and arrange them in such a way that the most favourable result can be expected.

<sup>2</sup> The auction terms shall be made available for public inspection at the debt enforcement office at least ten days before the auction.

### b. Contents

#### Art. 135

<sup>1</sup> The auction terms shall stipulate that immovable property be auctioned with all encumbrances (easements, real burdens, mortgages and registered personal rights) and that the associated personal debt obligations be transferred to the acquirer. The debtor of a transferred debt from a mortgage contract or mortgage certificate is released if the creditor does not declare within one year of the auction sale that he wishes to retain it (Art. 832 CC[^135-1]). Debts secured by mortgages that fall due are not transferred but paid in advance from the proceeds.[^135-2]

<sup>2</sup> The terms of the auction shall also specify the costs to be borne by the acquirer.

[^135-1]: [SR 210](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/5703)
[^135-2]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### c. Method of payment

#### Art. 136

[^136-1]

<sup>1</sup> The debt enforcement officer shall determine the method of payment in the auction terms; he may grant a payment term of a maximum of six months.

<sup>2</sup> Payment may be made in cash up to an amount of CHF 100,000. If the price is higher, the portion exceeding this amount must be paid through a financial intermediary in accordance with the Anti-Money Laundering Act of 10 October 1997[^136-2].

[^136-1]: Amended by No I 3 of the FA of 12 Dec. 2014 on the Implementation of the 2012 Revised Recommendations of the Financial Action Task Force, in force since 1 Jan. 2016 ([AS 2015 1389](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2015/289); [BBl 2014 605](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/2014/100)).
[^136-2]: [SR 955.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/11599)

### d. Payment deadline

#### Art. 137

[^137-1]

If a payment deadline is agreed, the immovable property remains under the administration of the debt enforcement office for the account and at the risk of the acquirer until the purchase price has been paid. In the meantime, no entry may be made in the land register without authorisation from the debt enforcement office. In addition, the debt enforcement office may require special security for the deferred purchase price.

[^137-1]: Amended by Art. 58 Final Title CC, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title CC; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).

### 3. Auction

### a. Announcement, registration of rights

#### Art. 138

<sup>1</sup> The auction shall be publicly announced at least one month in advance.

<sup>2</sup>

The announcement contains:

- 1\. place, date and hour of the auction;
- 2\. the indication of the day from which the auction terms are available for inspection;
- 3\.[^138-1] the request to the pledgees and all other parties to submit their claims to the immovable property, in particular for interest and costs, to the debt enforcement office within 20 days. This request must state that if this deadline is not met, they will only participate in the result of the realisation insofar as their rights are recorded in the land register.

<sup>3</sup> A corresponding request shall also be addressed to the owners of easements insofar as cantonal law is still applicable.[^138-2]

[^138-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
[^138-2]: Amended by Art. 58 Final Title CC, in force since 1 Jan. 1912 ([AS 24 233](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/24/233_245_233)Art. 60 Final Title CC; [BBl 1904 IV 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1904/4_1_1_), [1907 VI 367](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1907/6_367__)).

### b. Notification to the parties involved

#### Art. 139

[^139-1]

The debt enforcement office shall send a copy of the announcement by standard post to the creditor, the debtor, any third-party owner of the immovable property and all parties entered in the land register if they have a known place of residence or a representative.

[^139-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### c. Review of encumbrances, valuation

#### Art. 140

[^140-1]

<sup>1</sup> Prior to the auction, the debt enforcement officer shall determine the encumbrances (easements, real burdens, mortgages and registered personal rights) resting on the property on the basis of the submissions of the entitled parties and an extract from the land register.

<sup>2</sup> He shall send the list of encumbrances to the parties involved and at the same time set them a deadline of ten days to contest the list. Articles 106-109 apply.

<sup>3</sup> In addition, the debt enforcement officer shall order a valuation of the property and inform the parties concerned of the result.

[^140-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### d. Suspension of the auction

#### Art. 141

[^141-1]

<sup>1</sup> If a claim included in the list of encumbrances is disputed, the auction shall be suspended until the matter is settled if it may be assumed that the dispute will influence the sale price or that other legitimate interests will be harmed by a prior auction.

<sup>2</sup> If there is only a dispute about the nature of appurtenances or whether the appurtenances are only pledged to individual pledgees, the auction of the property together with the appurtenances may nevertheless take place.

[^141-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### e. Double auction

#### Art. 142

[^142-1]

<sup>1</sup> If a property is encumbered with an easement, a real burden or a registered personal right without the consent of the preceding mortgagee and the priority of the mortgage is evident from the list of encumbrances, the mortgagee may request the auction both with or without the encumbrance within ten days of service of the list of encumbrances.

<sup>2</sup> If the priority of the mortgage is not apparent from the list of encumbrances, the request for a double auction shall only be granted if the holder of the right concerned has recognised the priority or the mortgagee files an action for a declaration of priority within ten days of service of the list of encumbrances at the place where the property is located.

<sup>3</sup> If the offer for the property with the encumbrance is not sufficient to satisfy the creditor and he obtains better cover without it, he may demand the cancellation of the encumbrance in the land register. If a surplus remains after satisfaction, this shall be used primarily to compensate the entitled party up to the value of the encumbrance.

[^142-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 4. Sale. Coverage principle. Dispensing with realisation

#### Art. 142a

[^142a-1]

The provisions on the sale and the coverage principle (Art. 126) and on the waiver of realisation (Art. 127) apply.

[^142a-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 5. Consequences of default in payment

#### Art. 143

<sup>1</sup> If payment is not made on time, the sale shall be cancelled and the debt enforcement office shall immediately order a new auction. Article 126 applies.[^143-1]

<sup>2</sup> The former successful bidder and his guarantors shall be liable for the loss and any further damage. Interest shall be charged on the loss at five per cent.

[^143-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 6. Supplementary provisions

#### Art. 143a

[^143a-1]

Articles 123 and 132*a* also apply to the realisation of immovable property.

[^143a-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### 7. Sale by private contract

#### Art. 143b

[^143b-1]

<sup>1</sup> A sale by private contract may take the place of an auction if all parties involved are agree and at least the valuation price is offered.

<sup>2</sup> The sale may only take place after the proceedings to agree on the encumbrances has been carried out in accordance with Article 138 paragraph 2 number 3 and paragraph 3 and Article 140 and in corresponding application of Articles 135–137.

[^143b-1]: Inserted by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).

### D. Distribution of the proceeds

### 1. Time. Method

#### Art. 144

<sup>1</sup> The proceeds shall be distributed as soon as all the assets subject to distraint are realised.

<sup>2</sup> Assets may however be provisionally distributed beforehand.

<sup>3</sup> First of all, the costs of administration, realisation, the distribution of the proceeds and if applicable the purchase of a replacement item (Art. 92 para. 3) shall be paid from the proceeds.[^144-1]

<sup>4</sup> The net proceeds shall be paid out to the participant creditors up to the amount of their claims, including interest to the time of final realisation and the costs of debt enforcement (Art. 68).[^144-2]

<sup>5</sup> The amounts related to claims subject to provisional distraint are deposited temporarily in the official depositary.

[^144-1]: Amended by No I of the FA of 16 Dec. 1994, in force since 1 Jan. 1997 ([AS 1995 1227](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/1995/1227_1227_1227); [BBl 1991 III 1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/fga/1991/3_1_1_1)).
